In this report, \emph{safety-governed} means that eligibility is determined through explicit, non-compensatory checks derived from a versioned plant profile; it does not mean that the evaluator, model, or plant has been safety-certified. Release 0.1.0 is a public reference implementation for technical and research evaluation, not an authorisation for physical execution.

</details>


### [195] [Intertemporal Preference Steering in Qwen3 via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03892)
*Michal Mráz,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型Qwen3-32B中时间视野的线性表征，通过对比较探针识别短期与长期方向，并用对比激活加和（contrastive activation addition）对模型进行引导，实现了时间相关偏好的双向改变，并在跨分布的货币跨期选择任务和规划能力基准上验证了引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并控制大语言模型的时间视野（temporal horizon）对其在涉及延迟成本与收益的建议场景中至关重要。模型对短期与长期目标的偏好倾向会影响其提供建议的质量，同时长期规划的稳健性也是AI安全的重要问题。因此需要验证模型的时间偏好是否可测量、可引导。

Method: 首先在teacher-forced时间选择答案上训练对比线性探针（contrastive linear probes），在模型的残差流中找到短期与长期方向；然后使用对比激活加和（contrastive activation-addition）对模型进行引导，并在保留的二元时间选择任务、跨分布的货币跨期选择任务（变化奖励大小和延迟时间）以及TravelPlanner规划能力基准上评估引导效果。

Result: 研究发现简单对比线性探针即可识别时间视野方向，并可用于引导模型产生大幅度的双向偏好改变。在跨分布的货币选择任务中，引导显著双向移动模型对"更小更早"与"更大更晚"奖励之间的无差异阈值。此外，适度的时空引导还提升了与规划相关的能力指标。

Conclusion: 模型的时间跨期偏好是可测量且可引导的。这一发现对涉及延迟成本和收益建议的AI系统具有实际意义，同时也为长期规划相关的AI安全问题提供了研究基础。

Abstract: We study linear representations of temporal horizon in the large language model Qwen3-32B and use them to change the model's time-related preferences, recommendations, and capabilities. We train contrastive linear probes on teacher-forced temporal-choice answers to find a short-term versus long-term direction in the model's residual stream, and evaluate contrastive activation-addition steering on a held-out binary temporal-choice task, an out-of-distribution monetary intertemporal-choice task, and a TravelPlanner capability benchmark. The central result is that temporal-horizon directions can be identified with simple contrastive linear probes and then used for steering to induce large, bidirectional preference changes. On an out-of-distribution monetary choice task that varies reward size and delay, steering strongly shifts the model's indifference threshold between smaller-sooner and larger-later rewards in both directions. We further show improvements on a planning-related capability metric under moderate temporal steering. These results suggest that model intertemporal preferences are measurable and steerable, which is relevant for AI systems that give advice involving delayed costs and benefits, and for safety questions about long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [196] [When Efficiency Becomes Fragility: Exploiting Dynamic Routing Vulnerabilities in Adaptive UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03902)
*Shaofeng Liang,Runwei Guan,Wenshuo Chen,Jiemin Wu,Bowen Tian,Haozhe Jia,Kaishen Yuan,Songning Lai,Daizong Liu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了自适应Transformer跟踪器中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性，并基于此提出对抗性路径反转（API）框架，通过精确操控门控决策迫使推理进入错误计算路径，实现高隐蔽性、高效能的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机平台资源受限推动跟踪器从追求性能转向兼顾精度与效率，自适应Transformer跟踪器（利用输入依赖的动态路由架构）成为代表性解决方案。但作者发现这类架构存在关键的结构性缺陷：计算路径决策在离散跳层边界处具有无界的局部Lipschitz常数，导致微小输入扰动可在门控模块被放大，引发推理拓扑剧变，构成一个此前被忽视且高度可利用的拓扑路径空间攻击面。

Method: 作者在自适应跟踪架构的背景下形式化表征了这种Lipschitz奇异性，并首次将其识别为可直接利用的新型攻击面。在此基础上提出对抗性路径反转（API）框架：生成不可感知的扰动精确操纵门控决策，迫使推理进入被篡改的计算路径。利用原始路径与反转路径之间的严重不一致性，瓦解模型表示能力。

Result: 在SOTA自适应跟踪器上的大量实验表明，API在扰动隐蔽性、攻击有效性以及推理速度方面均达到更优性能。

Conclusion: 本工作为动态跟踪网络的安全分析开辟了新维度，并为未来构建鲁棒的自适应跟踪架构提供了理论警示。

Abstract: Resource constraints on UAV platforms have driven a paradigm shift in aerial tracking, from pursuing performance toward balancing accuracy with efficiency. Adaptive Transformer Trackers, which leverage an input-dependent dynamic routing architecture, have emerged as a representative solution to this challenge. However, we reveal that behind this computation-on-demand flexibility hides a critical structural flaw: the Lipschitz singularity of computational path decisions, which has an unbounded local Lipschitz constant at discrete layer-skipping decision boundaries. This mathematical discontinuity renders adaptive tracking networks inherently unstable: tiny input perturbations can be amplified at the gating modules, causing dramatic changes in the inference topology. We formally characterize this singularity in the context of adaptive tracking architectures and, for the first time, identify it as a directly exploitable new attack surface. This insight reveals a previously overlooked and highly vulnerable topological path space attack surface. Based on this, we propose the Adversarial Path-Inversion (API) framework. API generates imperceptible perturbations to precisely manipulate the gating decisions, forcing the inference onto altered computational paths. The severe inconsistency between the original and the inverted paths dismantles the representation capability of the model. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art adaptive trackers demonstrate that API achieves superior perturbation stealthiness, more effective attack, and faster inference speeds. This work opens a new dimension for the security analysis of dynamic tracking networks and provides a theoretical warning for constructing robust adaptive tracking architectures in the future.

</details>


### [197] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将多元对齐（pluralistic alignment）重新定位为社会学视角下的"社会性协调"问题，而非简单的输出多样化，并从社会学理论中提炼出角色结构、互动塑造和权力领域等概念资源，转化为AI系统设计的角色表示、结构化协调和情境敏感评估等具体方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统部署于日益多样化的社会场景，单一价值体系的对齐范式已不适用，需要支持多元合法视角。然而现有多元对齐方法缺乏对价值如何在实践中被社会组织、争论和协调的清晰理解，亟需理论支撑来指导系统设计。

Method: 本文通过引入社会学既有理论传统，将视角理解为由角色结构化、通过互动塑造、并在权力与专长领域中分布，进而将这些洞见转化为AI系统设计的具体含义，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调以及情境敏感评估。

Result: 论文提出一个设计空间，涵盖角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环等多层面的对齐要求，将多元对齐重新定位为社会性协调问题，并识别了未来在真实场景中实施和实证评估这些方法的研究方向。

Conclusion: 多元对齐应从输出多样化转向社会学视角下的社会性协调，通过结构化和可问责的方式让系统参与多元视角，对智能体系统而言还需对齐产出过程中的角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则与反馈循环等环节。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [198] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Álvarez和Ruggieri（2025）提出的因果感知（Causal Perception）理论框架，通过结构性和参数性两类因果感知的操作化，设计算法计算干预分布和反事实分布，并引入距离度量量化智能体间的分歧。在德国信用数据集上的多专家决策实验中，展示了因果感知如何影响准确率和公平性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知是一个有前景的概率框架，用于解释拥有竞争性结构因果模型（SCM）的智能体如何推断不同概率分布，但此前仅在理论层面被提出，缺乏实际实现和实证验证。

Method: 1) 将因果感知操作化为结构性（智能体对因果图存在分歧）和参数性（智能体对因果图一致但权重不同）两类。2) 设计计算干预分布和反事实分布的算法。3) 提出合适的距离度量来量化分歧。4) 在德国信用数据集上模拟多专家决策场景进行实证分析。

Result: 1) 因果感知的判定结果对距离度量和阈值的选择敏感。2) 因果感知会改变公平性评估和基于阈值的决策。3) 偏差相对于智能体的SCM具有情境性，证明公平性问题中竞争性世界观不可忽视。

Conclusion: 该工作将因果感知从纯理论概念推进到可计算、可实证的框架，揭示出在多专家决策场景中，不同智能体基于各自SCM产生的因果感知差异会实质性地影响准确率与公平性评估，表明公平性判断与认知者的因果模型密切相关。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [199] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对Transformer推理过程的新诠释：Transformer在推理时通过动态生成的参数来构建并应用提示词依赖的变换，被称为SIDPP（序列级交互动态并行处理）。该观点反驳了"随机鹦鹉"论断，认为LLM并非仅仅复现训练中的统计规律，而是通过输出权重的互联结构，让一些网络的输出决定其他网络的权重，从而动态构建变换并修改词元表征。作者还推测人类大脑皮层可能在原理上实现了类似SIDPP的计算形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"随机鹦鹉"观点（即认为大语言模型只是复现训练统计规律的论点），为Transformer的推理机制提供一个新的、可解释的理论框架，以期推动模型的可解释性、可控性及设计更小更可持续的系统，并探索与人类神经机制的联系。

Method: 从机制层面将Transformer重新概念化：token向量被视为被变换的概念，由矩阵和向量定义的参数化变换被视为变换的概念。区分静态变换（训练中固定）与动态变换（由输入序列生成）。在机制上，这些变换对应一组简单神经网络，Transformer的架构创新在于输出-权重互联（某些网络的输出决定其他网络的权重），而非仅有普通的输出-输入互联。

Result: 指出动态处理的贡献随提示词长度增长而增大，可能等于甚至超过静态处理的贡献，称之为"强提示敏感性"。该理论对可解释性、可预测性、可控性以及更小可持续系统的设计具有启示意义。

Conclusion: 由于人类神经系统具备实现SIDPP所需的机制，作者推测SIDPP可能在原则上可在大脑皮层中实现，进而猜想人类语言处理本身可能是一种SIDPP形式，其功能架构与Transformer具有相关性相似。

Abstract: This paper offers a new interpretation of the Transformer during inference. Against the "stochastic parrot" view that large language models merely reproduce statistical regularities learned in training, we argue that Transformers construct and apply prompt-dependent transformations whose parameters are generated during inference. We call this form of computation SIDPP: Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing. The Transformer is interpreted as a system that transforms concepts by means of concepts. Token vectors are the concepts to be transformed; parameterized transformations defined by matrices and vectors are the transforming concepts. These may be static, when fixed through training, or dynamic, when generated from the input sequence. Mechanically, they correspond to groups of simple neural networks. The Transformer's architectural novelty lies in output-weight interconnections, through which the outputs of some networks determine the weights of others, alongside ordinary output-input interconnections. By means of these interconnections, the system constructs transformations from the prompt and uses them to modify token representations. The contribution of dynamic processing grows with prompt length and may equal or exceed that of static processing, a phenomenon we call strong prompt sensitivity. This account bears on interpretability, predictability, control, and the design of smaller, more sustainable systems. Finally, since the human neural system possesses the mechanisms required to implement SIDPP, we argue that a form of SIDPP may, in principle, be neurally realized in the cerebral cortex. We therefore conjecture that human language processing may itself be a form of SIDPP produced by a functional architecture relevantly similar to that of the Transformer.

</details>


### [200] [TACT: Taxonomy-Aligned Post-Training for Pedagogically Adaptive English Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03952)
*Dongjie Yang,Siyan Lin,Leixian Shen,Rui Sheng,Huamin Qu,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TACT框架，通过两个互补的分类体系（Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy和Student-Move Taxonomy），构建了包含260段真实师生对话和32,379条标注的TACTCorpus语料库，并基于Qwen3.5-4B通过监督微调和分类对齐的GRPO后训练得到TACTutor，在TACTBench基准上超越所有被评估的专有基线模型，并在50名学习者的盲评中获得最高平均评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽被广泛用于ESL学习者对话练习，但有效的ESL辅导不仅需要流畅的回复生成，还需要根据学习者行为和对话语境选择恰当的教学动作。现有的人本辅导研究提供了适应性支持的原则，但这些原则通常针对特定任务，且未被充分整合到基于LLM的ESL辅导训练和评估中。

Method: 本文提出TACT框架：(1) 基于已有文献构建两个互补的分类体系——包含13种辅导策略的Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy和按动作类型及状态刻画学习者行为的Student-Move Taxonomy；(2) 利用分类体系构建TACTCorpus，丰富260段真实师生对话并添加32,379条标注和质量控制的增强训练数据；(3) 通过监督式微调加分类对齐的GRPO对Qwen3.5-4B进行后训练，生成TACTutor，优化重点是脚手架质量而非简单的参考模仿。

Result: 在TACTBench（包含78个真实辅导情境的策略均衡诊断基准）上，TACTutor相比其基础模型相对提升20.30%，并在相同协议下超越所有被评估的专有基线模型；在既有外部教育基准上保持基础模型性能；在50名学习者的盲评研究中获得最高总体平均评分。

Conclusion: 作者开源了数据、基准和模型权重，为开发教学自适应的ESL辅导系统提供了开放基础，验证了将教育学理论分类体系融入LLM后训练能够显著提升教学适应性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide conversational practice for English-as-a-second-language (ESL) learners. Effective ESL tutoring, however, requires more than fluent response generation: a tutor must select an appropriate pedagogical action based on learner behavior and dialogue context. Human-tutoring research offers principles for adaptive support, but they are often task-specific and remain insufficiently integrated into LLM-based ESL tutor training and evaluation. We present TACT (Taxonomy-Aligned Conversational Tutor), a human-grounded framework for post-training and evaluating pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors. Drawing on established literature, we develop two complementary taxonomies: the Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy with 13 tutor response strategies and the Student-Move Taxonomy characterizing learner behavior by move type and status. Using these taxonomies, we construct TACTCorpus, which enriches 260 authentic teacher-student conversations with 32,379 annotations and quality-controlled augmented training data. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization, producing TACTutor and optimizing it for scaffolding quality rather than reference imitation alone. On TACTBench, a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark comprising 78 authentic tutoring contexts, TACTutor improves over its backbone by 20.30% and outperforms all evaluated proprietary baselines under the same protocol, while maintaining backbone performance on established external educational benchmarks; in a blinded study with 50 learners, it also receives the highest overall mean rating among the evaluated tutors. We release the data, benchmark, and model weights, providing an open foundation for developing pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors.

</details>


### [201] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基础模型驱动的AI代理在经典社会困境中通过最优规划会收敛到稳定合作，与传统博弈论预测的相互背叛相矛盾。作者提出"嵌入式贝叶斯代理"理论模型，并引入"嵌入式均衡"作为替代纳什均衡的新解概念，为现代AI代理的社会行为提供基础博弈论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于基础模型的自主智能体日益融入社会和经济系统，理解其集体行为规律对于保障安全与合作至关重要。然而，经典博弈论基于"解耦代理"假设——将自身决策与环境和其他行动者视为独立——这一假设在现代AI代理（会联合预测自身未来行动与外部观测）中不再成立，因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 作者首先通过实验观察基础模型代理在风格化社会困境中迭代博弈的行为收敛模式；进而提出"嵌入式贝叶斯代理"理论模型：代理将自己视为所栖居宇宙的一部分，对自身决策算法保持认知不确定性；通过推断其他代理是否行为相似，代理将自己的规划推演视为证据——合作决策预测相似合作者的相似决策；最后形式化"嵌入式均衡"作为新的解概念。

Result: 关键发现是基础模型代理在进行最优规划时一致收敛到稳定合作，这与经典博弈论中"相互背叛"的预测直接矛盾；嵌入式贝叶斯代理模型能够解释这一现象，通过相似性推断机制说明合作如何从嵌入式代理的推理中涌现。

Conclusion: 该工作从"解耦代理"转向"嵌入式代理"视角，以嵌入式均衡取代纳什均衡，为理解现代AI代理的社会行为提供了新的理论基础，对AI安全与多智能体合作研究具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [202] [Interpretable Adaptive Sampling for LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03961)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于轻量级模糊控制器的自适应测试时扩展方法，通过可解释的信号（提示复杂度、模型置信度）动态分配每个查询的采样预算，在减少平均采样数的同时保持接近全预算基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理的测试时扩展使用固定每查询预算，在简单和困难提示上消耗相同计算量，且固定预算不透明，无法解释为何某个提示获得特定数量的样本。

Method: 设计轻量级模糊控制器，将可解释信号（估计的提示复杂度和模型置信度）映射到每查询采样预算；简单或高置信度提示分配较少样本，困难或低置信度提示分配较多样本，使推理计算可审查而非固定或不透明。在公平对齐协议下（匹配解码设置、受控答案选择）与best-of-N、计算感知缩放和基于自置信度的基线进行比较。

Result: 在问答和数学推理任务上，跨多个模型和数据集，自适应模糊控制优于多个标准基线，且与选择器匹配的全预算对照组性能接近，同时减少平均样本数。

Conclusion: 可解释的自适应采样是大语言模型测试时推理更高效的实际可行方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by generating and aggregating multiple candidate answers, yet many pipelines use fixed per-query budgets that spend the same compute on easy and difficult prompts. These fixed budgets are also difficult to inspect because they do not explain why a given prompt receives a particular number of samples. We propose adaptive} test-time scaling with a lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals, including estimated prompt complexity and model confidence, to a per-query sampling budget. The controller assigns fewer samples to easier or more confident prompts and more samples to harder or less certain prompts, making inference-time compute inspectable rather than fixed or opaque. We evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol with matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection, and compare against best-of-$N$, compute-aware scaling, and self-certainty-based baselines on question-answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across models and datasets, adaptive fuzzy control improves over several standard baselines and remains close to a selector-matched full-budget control while reducing the average number of samples. These findings suggest that interpretable adaptive sampling is a practical direction for more efficient test-time reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [203] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE（人类输入变异引擎），一个模拟语音转录和QWERTY键盘输入扰动（如语音不流畅、转录重构、键盘拼写错误）的测试套件，用于评估大语言模型对真实人类输入噪声的鲁棒性。研究发现：语音转录扰动显著降低所有指令微调模型的准确率，且代价来自转录结构而非填充词；键盘扰动成本更低且模型有较高容忍度；两类扰动的根本原因在于问题token的破坏数量而非新增token；两者差距仅体现在需要推理或构造答案的任务上；该损害不能仅归因于测试集污染，也无法通过轻量级适配消除；思维预算（thinking budget）几乎能完全恢复键盘通道但无法恢复语音通道，压缩语音甚至更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过打字或语音向语言模型输入信息，两种通道各自留下独特的噪声签名（键盘的拼写噪声、语音的转录不流畅和AI辅助转录的重构），但现有研究很少系统评估这些真实人类输入扰动对LLM性能的影响。

Method: 构建HIVE测试套件，包含一套语音转录扰动（如不流畅、填充词、转录重构）和一套QWERTY键盘扰动（如按键误触等拼写噪声），在多种指令微调模型上进行系统性鲁棒性评估，并通过消融实验分析扰动影响机制，如考察token存活率与准确率的关系、区分多选题与推理题、检验测试集污染、轻量级适配和思维预算的作用。

Result: 七大发现：（i）语音转录扰动全面降低模型准确率，代价主要来自转录结构而非填充词；（ii）键盘扰动代价较小，模型容忍度高；（iii）两类扰动的核心机制都是问题token的存活数量——破坏token才有伤害，新增token代价极低；（iv）两通道差距仅在需要构建或推导答案的任务中体现，多选题上无差异；（v）损伤不单纯源于测试集污染；（vi）轻量级适配无法消除该伤害；（vii）思维预算几乎完全恢复键盘通道但无法恢复语音通道，压缩语音反而更差。

Conclusion: LLM对两类人类输入通道的鲁棒性存在显著不对称性：对键盘噪声有较强缓冲能力，但对语音转录噪声（尤其是压缩语音）脆弱得多，且这种脆弱性难以通过轻量手段缓解，提示需要专门的鲁棒化策略或更好的语音转录前端。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>


### [204] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflectRL框架，将从更强专家模型失败产生的"Golden Negative Trajectories"（黄金负轨迹）用于策略训练的反思学习，而非简单丢弃。核心洞察是"Reflection Advantage"：对难题而言，反思一条有缺陷的轨迹比从零直接解题更容易且更有效。ReflectRL先利用这些轨迹引出反思推理，再通过反思到直接策略的迁移，将学到的推理行为转回直接推理。在9个基准、4种LLM骨干和4种在线策略训练方法上验证了一致性提升且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于专家黄金轨迹引导的在线策略训练方法在专家无法解决难题时会丢失主要监督来源，且通常将失败轨迹当作负样本直接丢弃。作者认为这些失败轨迹包含有价值的推理信号，不应作为模仿示范，而应作为可反思的有缺陷轨迹来利用，从而提出了从失败中学习的思路。

Method: ReflectRL包括两个阶段：(1) 利用Golden Negative Trajectories通过反思推理（Reflective Reasoning）激发模型对失败轨迹进行分析和修正；(2) 通过反思到直接策略迁移（Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition）将反思阶段学到的推理能力迁移回直接推理模式。该框架是轻量级、即插即用的，可嵌入多种在线策略训练方法。

Result: 在9个基准、4种LLM骨干和4种在线策略训练方法上的实验表明，ReflectRL能持续提升推理性能，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹并非无用，将失败作为反思对象可以成为在线策略训练的有效信号源；ReflectRL通过反思推理与策略迁移，在几乎不增加开销的情况下显著增强了模型推理能力，为后训练阶段利用失败数据提供了新范式。

Abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Micro-Segmentation Anomaly Detection in Zero-Trust Software-Defined Network Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02627)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨在零信任架构下，通过微隔离增强软件定义网络（SDN）中的异常检测。使用Vision Transformer（ViT）和1D卷积神经网络（1D-CNN）两种深度学习模型对比分析原始流数据与微隔离流数据，实验表明微隔离策略显著提升检测准确率（F1从0.90提升至0.95），其中ViT略优于1D-CNN，尤其在识别细微横向移动模式方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任架构（ZTA）强调严格的网络分段和持续验证，以减少隐式信任和横向威胁传播。然而传统粗粒度监控无法有效检测恶意行为，需要探索结合微隔离与深度学习的方法来增强SDN环境下的入侵检测效能。

Method: 在SDN系统中实施微隔离策略，构建两种深度学习异常检测模型：Vision Transformer（ViT）和1D卷积神经网络（1D-CNN）。将原始网络流数据与微隔离后网络流数据分别输入两种模型进行训练与测试，在模拟的零信任SDN数据集上进行对比实验。

Result: 实验结果表明微隔离显著提升检测性能：基于分割输入的模型在准确率和F1分数（F1=0.95）上优于未分割原始数据训练的模型（F1=0.90）。ViT模型整体表现略优于1D-CNN，尤其在识别未经处理数据中难以察觉的细微横向移动模式时优势明显。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在零信任网络中整合微隔离策略对提升入侵检测效能的重要性，并计划未来将方法扩展到更大规模的真实网络数据集，并探索动态在线分割技术。

Abstract: Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA) principles need rigorous network segmentation and ongoing verification to reduce implicit trust and lateral threat propagation. This paper investigates anomaly detection in software-defined networking (SDN) systems by micro-segmentation, using deep learning models to detect harmful actions that evade traditional coarse-grained monitoring. Two models are developed: a Vision Transformer (ViT) and a 1D Convolutional Neural Network (1D-CNN), which are used to both raw and micro-segmented network flow data. Experimental findings from a simulated zero-trust SDN dataset indicate that micro-segmentation substantially improves detection accuracy. The models trained on segmented input demonstrate enhanced accuracy and F1-scores (F1 = 0.95) compared to those utilizing unsegmented raw data (F1 = 0.90). The ViT-based detector marginally surpasses the 1D-CNN, particularly in recognizing nuanced lateral movement patterns that are unnoticed in unprocessed data. These findings highlight the significance of including micro-segmentation inside zero-trust networks to enhance intrusion detection efficacy. Future efforts will broaden this methodology to include extensive real-world network datasets and dynamic online segmentation techniques.

</details>


### [206] [Secure AI Watermarking Framework for IP Protection in Multi-Tenant Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02656)
*M Anjan Kumar,Kishor Kumar Gajula,Ch Prathima*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合AI水印和密钥分发认证的安全系统，用于多租户云AI服务环境中的数据防泄漏，通过主动和被动防御机制保护联邦学习与远程学习中的数据与模型交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于云的AI服务扩展，多租户环境中的私有认证平台面临数据泄漏风险，需要解决安全防护挑战。

Method: 提出结合密钥认证的AI水印系统，在可信方之间分发密钥，采用主动防御（基于算法域限制和受限访问）与被动防御（水印识别、生物特征识别、设备IP泄漏捕获）相结合的方法。

Result: 该方案能够在攻击发生前通过主动措施进行预防，并通过被动措施捕捉联邦学习和远程学习算法中数据和模型交换期间的泄漏事件。

Conclusion: 通过主动与被动相结合的安全机制，为多租户云AI环境提供更全面的数据安全防护。

Abstract: The Secured data safe guard transaction with multi-tenant environments run on private-protected authenticate platforms runs by secured handed environments that emerges with the expansion of cloud-based AI services. To enhanced this secured leakage address challenges solution to protect a secure AI Watermarking system incorporating key distributed between trusted parties based on key authentication as we proposed solution to guided safe guarded way to reactive, and proactive security alert systems using algorithms. This proposed system before attacks can be prevented through the active measures. domain run base restrictions with limited access. Conversely, Proposed system reactive methods to captured on watermarking and biometric identification owner device specific IP leakage that occur during the exchange of data and models in federated and remote learning algorithms.

</details>


### [207] [Your Agentic LLMs Secretly Encode Latent Signals of Indirect Prompt-Injection Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02657)
*Jianshuo Dong,Yiming Liu,Maosen Zhang,Nan Deng,Xu Peng,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了代理式大语言模型（agentic LLMs）在间接提示注入（IPI）攻击下的内部暴露机制。作者发现简单的线性探针可以在预生成隐藏状态上以90%以上的AUROC预测模型对IPI攻击的暴露程度，并提出了基于探针门控的推理防御方法AGRI，将攻击成功率大幅降低（如Qwen3.5-27B上从34.6%降至0%），同时引入了解释框架来分析探针捕获信号与自然语言解释之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式LLM容易受到间接提示注入攻击（恶意侧任务隐藏在外部工具结果中），但此前对LLM在IPI暴露时的内部机制知之甚少。作者希望深入理解模型内部如何在受攻击时编码相关信息，从而为防御提供可解释的、可探测的内部信号基础。

Method: 从三个维度展开研究：(1) 探测：在包括753B参数GLM-5.2在内的六个模型上，使用预生成隐藏状态训练简单线性探针预测IPI暴露，并评估其在未见攻击、指令和任务套件上的泛化性能，以及自适应攻击和跨语言场景下的鲁棒性；(2) 防御：通过思维链（CoT）测量揭示识别-行动差距，提出AGRI——一种探针门控的推理防御方法，仅按需（on demand）前置防注入推理；(3) 解释：构建分析框架，识别与探针捕获信号最相关的自然语言解释，分析不同模型间潜在信号与直接IPI暴露声明或间接操作线索的对应关系。

Result: 线性探针在六个模型上（包括753B参数的GLM-5.2）以90%以上AUROC预测IPI暴露，在未见攻击、指令和任务套件上保持泛化，且在自适应攻击和跨语言场景下鲁棒。AGRI在困难AgentDojo设置下大幅降低攻击成功率，例如在Qwen3.5-27B上从34.6%降至0%，同时基本保持干净任务效用。解释分析显示不同模型的潜在信号类型存在差异，可能与直接IPI暴露声明或间接操作线索对齐。

Conclusion: 通过探针探测内部隐藏状态可以可靠地揭示LLM的IPI暴露，且该信号可作为门控条件触发推理防御AGRI，显著提升安全性而几乎不损失正常任务性能。此外，不同模型对IPI信号的内部编码方式存在差异，为理解模型安全行为的可解释性提供新视角。代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic LLMs are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI) attacks, e.g., malicious side-tasks hidden in external tool results. While many efforts have sought to address the threats, little is known about the internals of agentic LLMs when they are exposed to IPI attacks, a condition which we call IPI exposure. In this paper, we study this problem in depth from three aspects. (1) Probing: Across six models, including the giant 753B-parameter GLM-5.2, simple linear probes trained on pre-generation hidden states can predict LLMs' IPI exposure. These probes achieve 90%+ AUROC on unseen attacks, agent instructions, and task suites; they exhibit high robustness under adaptive attacks and in cross-lingual settings. (2) Defense: Our CoT measurement reveals a recognition--action gap: though models encode such signals, they often fail to translate them into safe actions. We then introduce AGRI, a probe-gated reasoning-based defense that prepends anti-injection reasoning on demand. On difficult AgentDojo settings, AGRI substantially reduces attack success rate, e.g., from 34.6% to 0% on Qwen3.5-27B, while largely maintaining clean-task utility. (3) Explanation: We introduce an analysis framework that identifies natural-language explanations most strongly correlated with probe-captured signals. The resulting profiles differ across models: latent signals can align with either direct IPI-exposure claims or indirect operational cues. Code is available: https://github.com/jianshuod/IPI-exposure-signal.

</details>


### [208] [ZK-SR117: A Chunked Zero-Knowledge Attestation Design for Aggregated Fair-Lending Metrics, with a Control Mapping toward Full SR 11-7 Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02664)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Asaduzzaman Anik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分块零知识证明电路设计，用于在U.S.银行监管（SR 11-7和OCC 2011-12）框架下，对ML模型在受监管决策中的公平性与鲁棒性进行可审计证明，而无需暴露模型权重或客户数据。研究使用32,768行2022年HMDA抵押贷款真实数据，分32块各自独立生成并验证zkSNARK证明，最终聚合的公平统计量（demographic-parity gap）与真实持出值误差仅0.0029，每块证明时间小于4秒。同时扩展验证了期望校准误差（ECE）控制，误差仅0.00037。该研究还识别并修复了一个真实的数据质量问题，并提出了更完整的监管控制语言到零知识断言的映射方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管的决策场景（信贷承销、欺诈检测、贷款审批）中部署ML模型，需要向审计人员证明模型的公平性和鲁棒性，但不得泄露模型权重或客户数据。现有的美国银行监管框架（SR 11-7和OCC 2011-12）对模型验证提出了明确要求，而零知识证明提供了在不披露底层数据的情况下进行属性证实的途径，但现有电路设计在大规模数据上存在数值溢出或不可编译等可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出一种分块（chunked）零知识电路设计：将大规模数据集分割为多个批次（本文为32个块），每块独立生成zkSNARK证明，最后聚合各块的公平统计量。设计包括：(1) 对提交的、基于nonce采样批次数据进行demographic-parity gap的attested计算；(2) 相同的架构扩展至期望校准误差（ECE，10个bins）的第二项控制验证；(3) 提出基于nonce的采样协议以抵御银行端cherry-picking；(4) 将SR 11-7和OCC 2011-12的监管语言映射为九个零知识断言元素（涵盖soundness、calibration、robustness和drift）；(5) 提出部署威胁模型。研究还发现并解决了真实数据中的哨兵代码异常值问题，发布了预处理规范。

Result: 端到端实验在32,768行HMDA数据上完成：32个分块全部独立验证，demographic-parity gap的attested值与真实held-out值误差为0.0029，每块proving时间小于4秒。ECE控制项也在同一架构上完全验证，32块全部通过，每块proving约14.7秒，attested ECE与明文值误差仅0.00037。与平面求和电路（数千行即溢出）和树归约电路（数值精确但无法编译）相比，分块设计是唯一达到此规模且可行的方案。此外，识别并根因分析了一个真实数据质量问题（哨兵代码异常值），已通过公开发布的预处理规范解决。

Conclusion: 分块零知识电路设计是首个在真实监管数据集（HMDA 2022）上以可扩展方式演示公平性与校准控制的端到端方案，为受监管ML部署提供了可行的审计基础设施。但本文仅完整演示了两项控制、一种模型类别、一个任务；其余提出的映射（九项控制元素）、非ce采样协议和威胁模型属于设计工作而非已实现结果，留待未来工作。该研究同时强调了真实数据质量对零知识证明系统的影响，提示部署时需要将数据处理规范作为证明系统的一部分加以明确。

Abstract: Deploying ML models in regulated decision-making (credit underwriting, fraud detection, loan approval) requires demonstrating fairness and robustness to auditors without exposing model weights or customer data. We address this attestation problem for U.S. bank supervision under SR 11-7 and OCC 2011-12 guidance. We present a chunked zero-knowledge circuit design that attests an aggregated fairness statistic - the demographic-parity gap - on committed, nonce-sampled batches of real 2022 HMDA mortgage data, and demonstrate it end-to-end: 32,768 rows, 32 independently verified zkSNARK proofs, aggregated attested gap within 0.0029 of the true held-out value, per-chunk proving under 4 seconds. We also demonstrate extensibility by attesting a second control on the identical architecture - expected calibration error at 10 bins - with all 32 chunks verified, per-chunk proving at about 14.7 seconds, and attested ECE within 0.00037 of the plaintext value on the same committed rows. We compare this design against two alternatives - a flat summation circuit, which overflows past a few thousand rows, and a tree-reduction circuit, numerically exact but intractable to compile - and find the chunked design is the only one that reached this scale. We discovered and root-caused a genuine data-quality failure (a sentinel-code outlier distorting circuit proving and the fairness statistic itself) and resolved it with a published preprocessing specification. We also propose a fuller mapping from SR 11-7 and OCC 2011-12 control language to zero-knowledge statements (nine control elements spanning soundness, calibration, robustness, and drift), a nonce-based sampling protocol resisting bank-side cherry-picking, and a threat model for deployment, proposed as design work, not implemented results. Two controls, one model class, one task are demonstrated end-to-end; the rest is scoped and left as future work.

</details>


### [209] [Single Canonical Prompts Underestimate LLM Safety's Surface-Form Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02665)
*Yongxi Zhou,Junwei Yao,Yuanzhe Liu,Zihan Dong,Wenbo Ye,Jiaxi Wen,Lai Yun Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地检验了基准测试在单一"规范表面形式"下评估模型行为的忠实性问题，聚焦于安全这一高利害领域。通过预先编写的5种语义保持改写（机器反向翻译和语码混合生成器），对370个种子×5种表面形式×5个模型进行了受控实验，结果表明仅用规范形式评估会系统性低估不安全遵从行为：跨形式并集的危险结果比最差单一形式高出3.3-12.9个百分点，且5-13%在规范形式上安全的种子在某种改写下变得不安全，远高于零随机性底线。研究指出这种差异是模型依赖的，并给出了该形式集合的冗余性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数本质上是测量工具，但大多数基准只在单一规范表面形式上读取每个条目。这种阅读是否忠实值得质疑：当条目意图不变仅改变语义保持的表面形式时，规范形式的分数能否准确估计模型行为？在安全这一无金标可求的高风险场景下，这个问题的答案尤为关键。

Method: 研究采用预写改写以避免先验混淆——改写主要来自非大模型的机器反向翻译和矩阵语言框架语码混合生成器，确保每种表面形式对所有模型完全一致。所有响应由一个以人类为锚点、供应商中立的神谕法官（Claude，与人类在危险遵从上的kappa=0.86，跨语言稳定，并经GPT-4o交叉验证）评分，同时验证意图保持性。采用370个种子×5种表面形式×5个模型的设计，结合McNemar检验、bootstrap置信区间和XSTest良性对照组分析。

Result: 结果显示没有单一的改写变换在所有情况下最危险（20个逐变换McNemar检验中仅6个通过校正，且多数是保护性的）。但仅用规范提示会低估不安全遵从：所有形式并集的不安全结果超出最差单一形式3.3-12.9个百分点（所有5个模型的bootstrap 95%CI均排除零），5-13%的规范安全种子在某种改写下变得不安全——远高于零随机性底线（温度0下重采样5次规范形式未产生任何新的暴露）。这种差距是模型依赖的（Gemini 2.5 Pro上最大）。单一形式仅能恢复模型观察到的不安全表面的约53%，约三种形式能覆盖85%。良性对照组（XSTest）表明不稳定性是双向的。

Conclusion: 标准基准评估中单一表面形式的读法会系统性低估模型的不安全遵从行为，这一度量偏差具有统计学显著性和模型依赖性。研究建议评估需覆盖多种语义保持的改写形式而非依赖单一规范形式，并强调所观察的是具体形式集合的冗余性特征而非对整个改写总体的统计推断。

Abstract: A benchmark score is a measurement instrument, yet most benchmarks read each item at a single canonical surface form. We ask whether that reading is faithful: when an item's intent is held fixed and only its meaning-preserving surface form varies, does the canonical-form score estimate model behavior well, and how much of any variation is decoding/judge noise rather than signal? We instantiate this in safety, a high-stakes setting with no gold label to average toward. To avoid prior confounds, we pre-author the reformulations (refusal-free, mostly non-LLM: machine back-translation and a Matrix-Language-Frame code-switch generator) so an identical surface form reaches every model, score all responses with one human-anchored, vendor-neutral judge (Claude, kappa = 0.86 vs. human on unsafe compliance, stable across languages, cross-checked by GPT-4o), and verify intent preservation. On 370 seeds x 5 surface forms x 5 models, no single transformation is uniformly most dangerous (6 of 20 per-transformation McNemar tests survive correction, most protective). Yet evaluating only the canonical prompt underestimates unsafe compliance: the union of unsafe outcomes across forms exceeds even the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 pp, with bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero for all five models, and 5-13% of seeds safe on canonical are unsafe under some reformulation -- above a zero stochasticity floor (canonical resampled five times at temperature 0 gives 0/370 new exposures). The size of this gap is model-dependent (largest on Gemini 2.5 Pro). One form recovers only ~53% of a model's observed unsafe surface and about three reach 85% -- a redundancy characterization of this form set, not of a defined population. A benign control (XSTest) suggests the instability is bidirectional, though the benign and harmful pools are not item-matched. We release the dataset, code, and per-response labels.

</details>


### [210] [Vulnerabilities, Secrets and Misconfiguration in the Highest-Exposure Docker Hub Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02669)
*Cristhian Kapelinski,Beatriz Machado,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ChimangoScan管线，对Docker Hub全命名空间（1270万仓库、6638亿次拉取）进行大规模安全扫描（5.2万个高曝光仓库、6个独立扫描器、1.704亿条发现），揭示漏洞近乎普遍存在（96.3%镜像含已知包漏洞），且单工具报告在很大程度上是工具的产物（66.8%的漏洞-包组仅被一个漏洞扫描器标记），同时量化了秘密检测的误报（99.7%为非凭证）及zlib CVE的传播影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Docker Hub是大多数容器部署的底层注册表，基础镜像的缺陷会遗传给所有下游镜像。此前生态规模测量都依赖单一检测器，导致其计数未被量化的工具依赖性；而对比扫描器的研究仅用数十到数百张镜像的样本，缺乏大规模、多工具的对比验证。

Method: 构建ChimangoScan管线：1) 爬取Docker Hub全部命名空间（12,716,568个仓库）；2) 重建镜像层图（5440万条IS_BASE_OF边）；3) 通过曝光评分（镜像自身拉取数与其全部下游子树拉取数之和）对仓库排序；4) 对52,895个最高曝光仓库（占全部拉取的84.7%）用六种独立扫描器扫描，获得1.704亿条发现；5) 对手动标注的秘密检测进行验证分析。

Result: 漏洞近乎普遍：96.3%的镜像携带已知包漏洞，93.4%含严重漏洞，98.0%至少有一个CIS Docker Benchmark错误配置。在8070万个不同的（漏洞，包）组中，66.8%仅被三个漏洞扫描器之一标记，仅2.7%被三者都标记；最佳单扫描器仅恢复66.9%。TruffleHog在76.9%镜像中标记秘密，但1100个随机检测的人工标注显示99.7%为非凭证。单个zlib CVE达到的镜像携带总语料库47.3%的曝光，并传播到113万个不同下游镜像，但曝光度不能预测镜像的脆弱程度。

Conclusion: 容器生态的漏洞状况远比单一工具报告的更广泛也更不确定：单工具结论高度依赖工具选择，需要多扫描器交叉验证；秘密检测存在极高误报率，手动验证必不可少；依赖链传播影响巨大但曝光度不等于脆弱度，安全决策需结合更多维度，同时开源了管线与283 GB数据集供社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Docker Hub is the registry underneath most container deployments, and a flaw in a widely reused base image is inherited by every image built on it. Prior ecosystem-scale measurements each rely on a single detector, leaving the tool-dependence of their counts unquantified, while the studies that do compare scanners use samples of tens to hundreds of images. We present ChimangoScan, a pipeline that crawls the Docker Hub namespace (12,716,568 repositories, 663.8 billion cumulative pulls), reconstructs the image layer graph (54.4 million IS_BASE_OF edges), ranks images by an exposure score that folds an image's own pull count and those of its entire downstream subtree into one scalar, and scans the 52,895 highest-exposure repositories (84.7% of all recorded pulls) with six independent scanners, yielding 170.4 million findings. Vulnerabilities are near-universal: 96.3% of images carry a known package vulnerability, 93.4% a critical one, and 98.0% at least one CIS Docker Benchmark misconfiguration. The posture a single tool reports is largely an artifact of that tool: of 80.7 million distinct (vulnerability, package) groups, 66.8% are flagged by only one of the three vulnerability scanners and just 2.7% by all three, and the best single scanner recovers 66.9%. TruffleHog flags a secret in 76.9% of images, yet hand-labeling 1,100 random detections finds 99.7% are non-credentials. A single zlib CVE reaches images carrying 47.3% of total corpus exposure and propagates to 1.13 million distinct downstream images, but exposure does not predict how vulnerable an image is. We release the pipeline and the 283 GB dataset.

</details>


### [211] [Permission Denied: Policy-Graded Evaluation of Coding Agents in Hardened Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02670)
*Dotan Davidovich,Yair Amar,Hai Rozencwajg,Or Hiltch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Boundary-Bench，一个用于在安全策略约束下评估编码智能体的开源插件。研究在Terminal-Bench 2.1上测试了12个编码智能体，涵盖来自真实企业环境的嵌套安全策略层级，发现策略硬化总是有代价的：严格策略下成功率最高损失18.3个百分点，成本膨胀达167.3%，且成功率和效率两个维度并不一致，模型选择取决于具体策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准几乎都在宽松沙箱环境中评估，但实际部署中的智能体会受到企业安全控制（如受限凭据、受限出口、只读文件系统、非root执行）的约束。目前缺乏对这些约束如何影响智能体性能的系统性研究。

Method: 在Terminal-Bench 2.1上构建嵌套安全策略层级（源自真实企业常见限制），对12个编码智能体进行评估。同时验证严格策略下任务的可行性，区分模型失败与策略本身排除的任务，并分析策略阻塞时智能体的行为模式和失败分解。

Result: 硬化永远不是免费的，但代价远非均匀分布：最严格策略下成功率损失达18.3个百分点，成本膨胀167.3%。成功率和效率两个指标存在分歧——最能保持成功率的模型效率损失最大，模型选择与策略设置相关。失败模式分解显示，策略引发的失败多表现为超时或错误解决方案而非提前终止，具体混合因模型而异。作者还验证了严格策略下任务的可行性，将模型失败与策略限制导致的任务不可解分开。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的性能评估必须考虑真实安全策略约束；Boundary-Bench提供了一种可复现的方法来实现这种策略约束下的评估，并揭示了模型-策略之间复杂且非平凡的交互关系。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly run inside organizations whose security controls (scoped credentials, restricted egress, read-only filesystems, non-root execution) constrain them like any other software. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate agents almost exclusively in permissive sandboxes, so it is unknown how performance changes when policy is enforced. In this work, we evaluate 12 coding agents on Terminal-Bench 2.1 across nested security policy levels derived from common real-world enterprise restrictions. Hardening is never free but far from uniform: under the strictest policy, success losses reach 18.3 points and cost inflation 167.3\%, and the two axes disagree; the model that best preserves success is also the one that loses the most efficiency, so model choice is policy-dependent. Beyond aggregate scores, we characterize how agents behave when policy blocks their actions and decompose the failures hardening induces: runs grind into timeouts or wrong solutions rather than stopping early, in a mix that differs by model. To ground comparisons, we verify task solvability under the strictest policy, separating model failures from tasks the policy forecloses. We release Boundary-Bench, an open-source hardening plugin enabling policy-constrained evaluation of coding agents on Terminal-Bench and compatible benchmarks.

</details>


### [212] [On the Performance of Malware Detection Classifiers Using Hardware Performance Counters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02671)
*Alireza Abolhasani Zeraatkar,Parnian Shabani Kamran,Inderpreet Kaur,Nagabindu Ramu,Tyler Sheaves,Hussain Al-Asaad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用硬件性能计数器（HPC）和机器学习分类器进行运行时恶意软件检测，通过集成学习技术减少所需微架构事件数量，在仅用2个HPC的情况下性能优于使用8个HPC的标准分类器（提升达10%），且用4个HPC即可匹配使用16个HPC的标准ML检测器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的杀毒软件在恶意软件检测方面存在不足，基于硬件性能计数器（HPC）的硬件恶意软件检测器（HMD）成为有前景的补充方案。然而，处理器每个周期只能测量2到8个事件，导致需要多次运行应用程序才能收集足够的微架构事件，影响处理器效率。因此需要在不牺牲检测性能的前提下，减少所需HPC的数量。

Method: 提出基于HPC和机器学习分类器的恶意软件检测方法，采用集成学习（ensemble learning）技术来提升硬件恶意软件检测器的性能，从而减少必要的微架构事件数量。实验使用了18种机器学习模型和2种集成学习方法，共构成144种不同配置进行性能评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于集成学习且仅使用2个HPC的恶意软件检测方法，优于使用8个HPC的标准分类器，性能提升达10%；仅需4个HPC即可匹配使用16个HPC的标准ML检测器的性能，从而实现了有效的运行时恶意软件检测。

Conclusion: 集成学习技术能够有效减少硬件恶意软件检测所需的HPC数量，在保证甚至提升检测性能的同时，消除多次运行应用程序的需求，提高处理器效率，使运行时恶意软件检测更加切实可行。

Abstract: Malware detection using Hardware Performance Counters (HPC) has emerged as a promising solution to improve the security of computing systems as a complement to antivirus software. Hardware-based malware detectors (HMD) use Machine Learning (ML) classifiers to detect malicious application patterns. The inputs to ML classifiers are low-level performance features known as HPCs, hardware-related activity data collected from a processor at run time to profile the low-level microarchitectural behavior of an application. This paper proposes malware detection using HPCs and machine learning classifiers and highlights the effectiveness of malware detection at run-time. We use ensemble learning techniques to improve the performance of the hardware-based malware detectors, which reduces the number of necessary micro-architectural events. This improves the processor's efficiency by eliminating the need to run an application several times since a processor can measure only 2 to 8 events at a cycle. We use 18 machine-learning models along with two ensemble learning methods to evaluate the malware detection performance, creating a total of 144 different configurations. The experimental results show that the ensemble learning-based malware detection with 2 HPCs using the ensemble technique outperforms standard classifiers with 8 HPCs by up to 10%. It also matches the performance of standard ML-based detectors that use 16 HPCs while requiring only 4 HPCs, thereby enabling effective run-time malware detection.

</details>


### [213] [Security-First Evaluation of Text-to-Terraform: Benchmarking LLMs and SLMs for Secure IaC Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02672)
*Francis Luis Santos Vargas,Rodrigo Brandão Mansilha,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文档评估了七种LLM和SLM模型（三种闭源LLM和四种开源SLM）在AWS Terraform生成中的安全合规能力，通过Checkov和Trivy扫描发现：语法有效性与安全合规性在很大程度上正交，提示工程本身不足以保证安全，需自动化多工具扫描作为必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云配置错误是安全事件的首要原因，但LLM/SLM能否生成符合安全规范的IaC（基础设施即代码）仍是开放问题。需要系统性评估和基准测试揭示模型与提示策略对安全合规性的影响。

Method: 在17个AWS Terraform生成场景中基准测试七种模型（Claude Opus 4, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 Pro等闭源LLM；Qwen2.5-Coder-14B, WizardCoder-33B等开源SLM），集成Checkov和Trivy扫描器到GitLab CI/CD流水线，评估两种提示策略在三个安全级别下的pass@5表现。

Result: WizardCoder-33B达到77.8%的验证率但Checkov合规率为零；Claude Opus 4在详细安全提示下达到23.1%的Checkov和92.5%的Trivy通过率。语法有效性与安全合规性高度正交，不同模型在两者间表现出显著分化。

Conclusion: 提示工程本身不足以确保生成IaC的安全合规性，自动化多工具扫描（如Checkov与Trivy联合）是LLM辅助IaC生成的必要补充，无论模型家族或提示策略如何。所有测试工件已公开。

Abstract: Cloud misconfiguration remains a leading cause of security incidents, yet whether LLMs and SLMs can generate security-compliant Infrastructure-as-Code is an open question. We benchmark seven models, three closed LLMs (Claude Opus 4, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 Pro) and four open SLMs (Qwen2.5-Coder-14B, WizardCoder-33B, CodeLlama-13B, Magicoder-S-CL-7B), on AWS Terraform generation across 17 scenarios, integrating Checkov and Trivy scanners into a GitLab CI/CD pipeline and evaluating two prompt strategies at three security levels (pass@5). Syntactic validity and security compliance are largely orthogonal properties in LLM-generated IaC, a model that reliably produces well-formed Terraform does not necessarily produce secure Terraform: WizardCoder-33B achieves 77.8% validate rate yet zero Checkov compliance, while Claude Opus 4 reaches 23.1% Checkov and 92.5% Trivy pass rates under detailed security prompting. Consequently, prompt engineering alone is insufficient: automated multi-tool scanning remains a necessary complement to LLM-assisted IaC generation regardless of model family or prompt strategy. All artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [214] [Moving the Safety Barrier: Dynamic Routing Adaptive Alignment Against White-Box Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02674)
*Shangze Li,Chuancheng Shi,Simiao Xie,Lingzhi He,Cheng Ji,Zifeng Cheng,Fei Shen,Chao Wu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出动态路由自适应对齐（DRAA）框架，通过识别安全路由并构建补偿路由来抵抗针对模型安全神经元或路由的白盒攻击，在多种攻击下提升鲁棒性且不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御依赖静态安全单元或固定拒绝路径，面对目标性的路由级白盒攻击时高度脆弱；随着大模型在开放环境中的部署，攻击威胁正从黑盒越狱转向能够直接定位并破坏内部安全神经元的白盒攻击，亟需更鲁棒的安全机制。

Method: 首先通过对安全与不安全校准样本的内部激活进行对比，定位模型的"安全路由"；然后掩蔽该安全路由以诱导因果性防御失败案例，再从中选择性地挖掘防御失败样本，构建失败感知的偏好对（failure-aware preference pairs），最终通过动态补偿路由实现安全行为的重构与加固。

Result: 大量实验表明，DRAA能有效重构模型安全性的底层通路依赖，显著提升对路由级白盒攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持模型的一般实用性不下降。

Conclusion: DRAA通过动态补偿路由替代静态安全机制，为应对路由级白盒攻击提供了一种有效的安全对齐新范式，在鲁棒性与通用性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of large foundation models (LFMs) in open environments, safety threats are shifting from black-box jailbreaks toward white-box attacks that directly identify and disrupt internal safety neurons or routes. However, existing safety defenses often rely on static safety units or fixed refusal pathways, leaving models highly vulnerable to targeted route-level white-box attacks. For that, we propose dynamic routing adaptive alignment (DRAA), a framework that introduces dynamic compensatory routes to preserve robust refusal behavior when the safety route is compromised. Specifically, we first identify and localize the model's safety route by contrasting internal activations between safe and unsafe calibration samples. DRAA then masks this safety route to induce causal failure cases and selectively mines the resulting defense failures, thereby constructing failure-aware preference pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DRAA effectively restructures the underlying pathway dependence of model safety, substantially improving robustness against route-level white-box attacks, while preserving general utility.

</details>


### [215] [DenialRAG: Single-Document RAG Poisoning via Embedded Parametric Denial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02678)
*Abay Zhurekbay,Tao Liu,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DenialRAG，一种单文档投毒攻击方法，通过在检索语料中插入包含"明确指出正确答案→否认它→给出错误答案的解释"结构的恶意文档，将冲突直接嵌入生成器所见的上下文中，从而诱导LLM产生攻击者想要的错误答案。实验表明该方法在多个模型和数据集上表现优异，但攻击有效性高度依赖目标模型，且现有防御无法提供统一保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG语料投毒攻击通常避免在恶意文档中明确提及和反驳正确答案。作者希望探索一种互补的攻击设计——在同一个被检索的段落中同时呈现正确答案和攻击者构造的错误答案及解释，将冲突直接嵌入生成器上下文，从而实现更有效的攻击。

Method: DenialRAG攻击的核心是在单文档中构建三段式结构：先明确写出正确答案，然后否认该答案，最后给出攻击者控制的、支持错误答案的解释。通过将正确与错误答案同时置于同一被检索段落中，使冲突直接暴露在生成器的上下文中。评估涵盖三个开放域QA数据集、四家供应商的八个目标LLM、四种已发表的单文档攻击基线以及五种推理时防御方法。

Result: DenialRAG在全部三个Mistral-7B数据集上取得最高攻击成功率(ASR)，在若干个其他LLM上也保持有效性，但部分模型上其他攻击方法占优。防御实验显示ASR有显著降低但不均匀，每种防御在部分设置下仍有残留ASR。组件级和跨模型分析表明"否认"部分是最有影响力的组件，不同投毒机制在不同模型组中失效速度不同。

Conclusion: RAG投毒风险无法由单一攻击家族或单一目标模型全面刻画。攻击有效性高度依赖于目标模型架构，防御的保护效果也不统一，说明需要针对不同模型与攻击组合进行细粒度的安全评估与防御设计。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to corpus poisoning: an attacker who inserts a crafted document into the retrieval corpus can steer the underlying large language model (LLM) toward an attacker-chosen wrong answer. Prior single-document attacks typically avoid explicitly naming and refuting the correct answer inside the poisoned passage. In this paper, we examine a complementary design and propose \emph{DenialRAG}, a single-document poisoning attack that explicitly names the correct answer, denies it, and presents an attacker-controlled explanation for favoring the wrong answer. By placing both the correct answer and the corresponding poisoned answer inside the same retrieved passage, DenialRAG embeds the conflict directly into the context seen by the generator.
  We evaluate DenialRAG against four published single-document poisoning attacks across three open-domain question-answering datasets, eight target LLMs from four vendors, and five inference-time defenses. The results show that attack effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: DenialRAG achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR) on all three Mistral-7B datasets and remains effective on several other target LLMs, while other attacks dominate in some model regimes. Defense results show meaningful ASR reductions but non-uniform protection, with each defense leaving residual ASR in some settings. Component-level and cross-model analyses further identify the embedded denial as the most influential tested component and show that different poisoning mechanisms lose effectiveness at different rates across model groups. Together, these results show that RAG poisoning risk cannot be fully characterized by a single attack family or a single target model.

</details>


### [216] [Safety in Batches? Understanding and Mitigating Safety Failures in Batch Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02681)
*Kihyun Kim,Hee-Seon Kim,Wonjun Lee,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个新的安全漏洞：批处理提示（batch prompting）在提升大语言模型实用性的同时，会削弱其安全性——一个单独提问时会被可靠拒绝的有害问题，嵌入一组良性问题中却可能诱导出有害回答。作者将其确认为独立的安全失败模式，并提出批量感知偏好优化（batch-aware preference optimization）作为缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理提示是一种实用的大语言模型推理策略，但其安全性影响尚未被充分探索。研究者希望填补这一盲区，揭示批处理策略在提升效用的同时是否削弱了模型的安全对齐。

Method: 将有害问题嵌入一组良性问题中进行批处理提示攻击；从对齐信号减弱（alignment signal weakening）和拒绝信号稀释（refusal signal dilution）两个互补视角分析根本原因；在多种开源和前沿商业模型上进行黑盒攻击测试；提出批量感知偏好优化（batch-aware preference optimization）作为防御手段。

Result: 实验表明，批处理提示在多种广泛使用的开源及前沿商业模型上均能实现较高的攻击成功率，且该漏洞无法归因于已知的上下文学习或长上下文效应；批量感知偏好优化能有效缓解该漏洞。

Conclusion: 批处理提示的安全失效模式暴露了当前安全对齐中的一个盲点，批量感知对齐（batch-aware alignment）是确保模型稳健部署的必要方向。

Abstract: Batch prompting is a practical inference strategy for large language models, but its safety implications remain underexplored. We show that the success of batch prompting for utility does not extend to safety: a harmful question that is reliably refused in isolation can elicit a harmful response when embedded in a batch of benign questions. We identify this as a distinct safety failure mode, not reducible to known vulnerabilities such as in-context learning or long-context effects, and analyze its causes from two complementary perspectives: alignment signal weakening and refusal signal dilution. Across widely used open-source and frontier commercial models, batch prompting consistently achieves high attack success rates as a simple black-box attack. We further show that batch-aware preference optimization effectively mitigates the vulnerability. These findings highlight a blind spot in current safety alignment and point to batch-aware alignment as a necessary step toward robust deployment.

</details>


### [217] [$S^3$: Improving Agent Safety through Multi-Stage Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02683)
*Zibo Xiao,Haoyu Wang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出S³，一种基于"阶段特定安全技能"（Stage-Specific Safety Skills）的统一抽象，将异构安全设计转化为可复用、可组合的组件，并通过守卫智能体（guard agent）在多阶段智能体工作流中进行风险检测与缓解。实验表明S³在安全有效性和效用保持上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖多阶段工作流（记忆、规划、工具执行等）完成复杂任务，但风险可能在不同阶段出现、跨步骤传播，难以检测和缓解。现有安全方法仅保护孤立阶段，难以集成，缺乏对整个工作流的全面防护。

Method: 提出Stage-Specific Safety Skills统一抽象，将异构安全设计表示为具有明确阶段语义的可复用可组合组件；开发自动化转换流水线将现有安全设计转化为安全技能；建立社区驱动的安全技能库；在此基础上提出S³多阶段防御框架，由一个守卫智能体编排阶段特定安全技能进行风险检测与缓解；构建多阶段风险基准（MSRB）评估跨阶段代表性风险。

Result: 在MSRB基准上，S³在安全有效性和效用保持两方面均一致优于代表性最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 阶段特定安全技能可作为构建可扩展、可组合的韧性与可信赖智能体系统的基础，为多阶段工作流提供全面防护。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on multi-stage agentic workflows, with stages such as memory, planning, and tool execution, to accomplish complex tasks. However, risks may emerge at different stages, propagate across steps, and become difficult to detect and mitigate. Existing safety methods protect only isolated stages and are difficult to integrate, leaving agents without comprehensive protection throughout the workflow. To address these limitations, we introduce Stage-Specific Safety Skills, a unified abstraction that represents heterogeneous safety designs as reusable and composable components with explicit stage semantics. We further develop an automated transformation pipeline that converts existing safety designs into reusable safety skills and establish a community-driven safety skill library. Building on this abstraction, we propose $S^3$, a multi-stage defense framework in which a guard agent orchestrates stage-specific safety skills for risk detection and mitigation throughout the agentic workflow. We also construct the Multi-Stage Risk Benchmark (MSRB) to evaluate representative risks across workflow stages. Experimental results show that $S^3$ consistently outperforms representative state-of-the-art baselines in both safety effectiveness and utility preservation. These results demonstrate the potential of stage-specific safety skills as a scalable and composable foundation for building resilient and trustworthy agent systems.

</details>


### [218] [PolicyGuard: Prompt-Configurable Semantic DLP for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02687)
*Kyutae Park,Jungwon Kim,Daeyeol Shim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolicyGuard是一个面向AI编码代理的预模型数据丢失防护（DLP）拦截框架，通过"策略即提示"（policy-as-prompt）范式，使用自然语言策略文件引导LLM对用户提示进行分类，在冻结测试集上达到96.5%的有效拦截率和3.0%的误报率，并支持跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DLP解决方案在应对AI编码代理场景时存在明显不足：基于正则表达式的方案过于僵硬，模型微调成本高且不灵活，供应商管理的分类器缺乏可定制性。用户可能通过自然语言提示无意中泄露凭据、个人身份信息（PII）或专有业务数据，亟需一种可定制、无需重训练且部署灵活的DLP方案。

Method: PolicyGuard采用预模型拦截架构，核心创新包括：(1) policy-as-prompt范式，将DLP分类标准完全定义在可由非工程人员编辑的纯文本文档中，无需代码修改或模型重训练；(2) 密封评估协议，采用模板族级别的数据划分、隐藏留出集和冻结测试集来严格评估泛化能力；(3) 基于LLM的自然语言策略引导分类，而不是传统的正则模式或微调分类器。

Result: 在2,000条多语言提示的评估中，冻结测试集（927条提示）上实现了96.5%的有效拦截率（EBR）和仅3.0%的误报率（FPR）；在217条提示的隐藏留出集上达到100%准确率。信息匹配基线实验表明，自然语言格式显著优于等效JSON格式（McNemar卡方=31.58，p<0.001），且大幅优于零样本分类（Cohen's h=0.915）。跨模型可移植性实验显示，同一策略在四个不同LLM上无需修改即可实现86.4%-96.5%的EBR。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard证明了基于自然语言策略文件的LLM引导DLP框架在AI编码代理场景中具有高准确率、低误报率、强大泛化能力以及跨模型可移植性，为非工程师提供了可编辑的DLP策略管理方案，是该领域的有效方法。

Abstract: AI coding agents accept free-form natural language prompts that may inadvertently contain credentials, personally identifiable information (PII), or proprietary business data. Existing data loss prevention (DLP) solutions rely on rigid regex patterns, model fine-tuning, or vendor-managed classifiers with limited customizability. We present PolicyGuard, a pre-model interception framework that classifies user prompts using an LLM guided by a natural language policy file. Our key contributions are: (1) the policy-as-prompt paradigm, where DLP classification criteria are defined entirely in a plaintext policy document editable by non-engineers without code changes or model retraining; (2) a sealed evaluation protocol with template-family-level data splits, hidden holdouts, and frozen test sets to rigorously assess generalization; and (3) a comprehensive empirical evaluation across 2,000 multilingual prompts demonstrating 96.5% effective block rate (EBR) with only 3.0% false positive rate (FPR) on a frozen test set of 927 prompts, and perfect 100% accuracy on a 217-prompt hidden holdout. Information-matched baseline experiments show that PolicyGuard's natural language format significantly outperforms equivalent content in JSON format (McNemar chi-squared = 31.58, p < 0.001) and dramatically outperforms zero-shot classification (Cohen's h = 0.915). Cross-model portability experiments demonstrate that the same policy achieves 86.4-96.5% EBR across four different LLMs without modification.

</details>


### [219] [Stylometric Defenses Against Author Impersonation in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02695)
*Leonid Ravich,Michael Fire*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将风格计量分析应用于补丁级提交，通过微调跨模态Transformer在20余年Linux内核提交历史上学习代码差异与提交消息的统一风格嵌入，实现开放世界作者身份验证（ROC AUC 0.93），并部署为适配CI/CD的流式异常检测器。在2021年PHP后门和2026年ForceMemo/GlassWorm两次供应链攻击的回顾性验证中，无需重训练即可将伪造提交排在审计队列前约1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链攻击日益利用身份漏洞，即被攻破的维护者账户授权了恶意代码变更。传统的防御手段难以发现这类"合法身份、恶意行为"的攻击，因此需要一种行为层面的防御层来验证补丁是否确实出自其声称的作者。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 跨模态Transformer模型，在20余年Linux内核提交历史上微调，将代码差异（diff）和提交消息编码到统一的风格计量空间中，用于补丁级作者身份验证；(2) 基于该嵌入的流式异常检测器，适配持续集成/持续部署（CI/CD）环境的在线检测需求。

Result: 开放世界作者身份验证任务上达到ROC AUC 0.93。在两项真实供应链攻击的回顾性验证中：2021年PHP后门攻击的伪造提交被定位在维护者审计队列的约前1%；2026年ForceMemo/GlassWorm活动的28个可评分伪造提交的每仓库中位审查负担为0.8%。

Conclusion: 跨模态补丁级嵌入能够支持对真实世界仓库中作者冒充行为进行行为层面的分流（triage），在无需重训练的情况下即可有效覆盖不同补丁特征的供应链攻击场景，为维护者审计提供了可行的优先排序信号。

Abstract: Software supply-chain attacks increasingly exploit an identity gap where compromised maintainer accounts authorize malicious changes. This work evaluates patch-level authorship verification as a behavioral defense layer, showing that stylometric analysis can operate not only on full source files but also on patch-level commits. We fine-tune a cross-modal transformer on more than 20 years of Linux kernel commit history to embed code diffs and commit messages into a unified stylometric space, achieving ROC AUC of 0.93 for open-world authorship verification. We then use these representations in a streaming anomaly detector suited to continuous integration and deployment (CI/CD) settings. We validate the pipeline on two retrospective supply-chain incidents involving different patch characteristics: the 2021 PHP backdoor and the 2026 ForceMemo/GlassWorm campaign. Without retraining, the proposed detector surfaces both PHP forged commits within approximately 1% of the maintainer audit queue and ranks the 28 scoreable ForceMemo spoofs with a median per-repository review burden of 0.8%. These results indicate that cross-modal patch-level embeddings can support behavioral triage against author impersonation in real-world repositories.

</details>


### [220] [Steganalysis of Adaptive Covert Collusion in Tool-Using Agent Populations: A Black-Box, Cross-Principal Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02698)
*Mohamed Chahine Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对基于LLM的工具型智能体在共享基础设施上大规模部署带来的"群体级隐蔽协调"风险，提出了一种黑盒隐写检测器。该检测器仅基于行为（轨迹/工具调用/时序）工作，并结合交叉运行互信息估计、置换检验、分布偏移统计以及时序和工具调用侧信道，在固定误报预算下校准。核心创新在于将对抗方视为"持续改写编码"的适应性对手，通过红蓝对抗在真实工具使用环境中绘制"检测-容量边界"，并从容量理论推导出黑盒检测失效的隐蔽比特率下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体安全防护无法应对群体层面的协调攻击——多个"表面合规"的智能体可以悄然串通操纵市场、操纵评审或协同窃取数据，而组织之间无法查看彼此的模型，因此现实可行的检测器只能基于行为（黑盒、仅轨迹、部分可见）工作。这一群体性风险构成了现有单智能体安全框架的盲区。

Method: 将隐蔽协调建模为信息隐藏问题，构建黑盒隐写分析检测器，融合四类信号：(1) 跨运行互信息估计，(2) 置换检验，(3) 分布偏移统计，(4) 时序与工具调用侧信道，并在固定误报率预算下标定。核心方法是将传统"对固定编码检测"升级为"对抗持续改写编码的对手"，在带工具调用和记忆的真实环境中进行红蓝对抗，并以容量理论推导检测-容量边界及检测失效的隐蔽比特率下界。

Result: 论文报告了带明确标注的占位符实验结果（pending measurement），并识别出一个实用性规避手段——将负载跨会话（session）拆分传播，现有方法难以捕捉。实验设计旨在测绘检测-容量边界。

Conclusion: 结论指出：基于容量的理论分析表明，存在一个隐蔽比特率下界，低于此速率时黑盒检测在统计意义上不优于随机猜测（"no better than chance"）；同时强调跨会话负载拆分是一种当前方法普遍遗漏的实际规避途径，需要后续工作关注。

Abstract: Tool-using agents built on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed not by a single operator but by many, side by side on shared infrastructure. This creates a population-level risk that single-agent safeguards miss: a handful of agents can quietly coordinate, rigging a market, boosting one another in a review process, or timing a joint data grab, while each one looks perfectly well-behaved. The difficulty is that the organisations running these agents cannot see inside one another's models, so any realistic detector must work from behaviour alone: black-box, trace-only, and often with only partial visibility. We treat covert coordination as an information-hiding problem and build a black-box steganalysis detector that combines cross-run mutual-information estimation, permutation tests, distributional-shift statistics, and timing and tool-call side channels, all calibrated to a fixed false-positive budget. Our central move is to stop testing against a single fixed code: we pit the detector against an adversary that continually rewrites its encoding to slip past whatever the detector has learned, and we run this red-versus-blue contest in tool-using, memory-carrying environments rather than toy games. Capacity theory then tells us what to expect, a detection-capacity frontier, a covert bit-rate below which black-box detection is provably no better than chance. We set out an experiment to map this frontier, report clearly labelled placeholder results pending measurement, and flag a practical evasion, spreading a payload across sessions, that current methods largely miss.

</details>


### [221] [Privacy-Preserving AI Verification via Minimal Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02774)
*Sleem Abdelghafar,Gabriel Kulp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出最小信息披露（MID）框架，用于设计并量化AI验证过程中面向验证者证据的信息内容，解决"验证需要足够信息以建立授权声明，但同一证据可能泄露模型、工作负载或硬件敏感细节"的核心矛盾。MID通过条件互信息度量附带泄露，并支持不同验证目标、受保护属性、证据来源及部署约束，并通过实验展示了完美验证与零附带泄露的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI验证跨越了信任边界：验证者必须学习足够信息以确认授权声明，但同样的证据可能泄露关于模型、工作负载或硬件的敏感细节。现有方法缺乏对验证者面向证据本身信息内容系统性的设计和量化手段，无法在验证需求与隐私保护之间取得平衡。

Method: MID框架以条件互信息度量附带泄露（即在已知授权结果后，所释放信息关于受保护属性所揭示的内容）。该框架具有通用性，可适配不同验证目标、受保护属性、证据来源和部署约束。实验涵盖四种物理测量和六个验证任务（执行类型、硬件身份、计算规模、模型身份），使用三个机制设计变量（证据通道、收集策略、释放变换），并支持ZKP认证释放（使用Groth16 zk-SNARK实现线性投影机制）。

Result: 在四个物理测量和六个验证任务上，MID产生了三种具有完美保留验证（held-out）和零测量附带泄露的释放，其余任务则得出明确的隐私-效用边界。同时，MID能够支持基于zk-SNARK的零知识证明认证释放。

Conclusion: MID为AI验证中的信息泄露问题提供了系统化的设计和量化框架，实证表明在多种验证任务中可以实现完美验证与零附带泄露的兼顾，并可与零知识证明技术协同工作，为隐私保护型AI验证提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: AI verification crosses a trust boundary: a verifier must learn enough to establish an authorized claim, yet the same evidence can reveal sensitive details about the model, workload, or hardware. We introduce minimal information disclosure (MID), which designs and quantifies the information content of verifier-facing evidence itself. MID measures collateral leakage with conditional mutual information: what the release reveals about the protected property after the authorized result is known. MID is general by design: it can accommodate different verification goals, protected properties, evidence sources, and deployment constraints. To demonstrate MID's practicality, we evaluate it on four physical measurements and six verification tasks spanning execution type, hardware identity, compute scale, and model identity. These experiments use three mechanism-design variables--the evidence channel, collection policy, and release transformation--but MID is not limited to these choices and can accommodate other deployable mechanisms. Across these tasks, MID produces three releases with perfect held-out verification and zero measured collateral leakage, while the remaining tasks yield explicit privacy--utility frontiers. MID also supports ZKP-certified releases: we demonstrate our proposed linear-projection mechanism using a Groth16 zk-SNARK.

</details>


### [222] [Fast Object Removal Attacks on Safety-Critical Video-based Perception Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02806)
*Mohammad Imtiaz Hasan,M Sabbir Salek,Nathan Jones,Mashrur Chowdhury,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基于视频的智能交通系统（ITS）的近实时定向目标移除攻击框架，通过四阶段端到端管线（目标定位、连贯补丁检索、上下文感知alpha合成、帧重建）实现对YOLO等检测器的有效攻击，在真实交叉路口场景中达到97.59%的检测率降低和94.48%的攻击成功率，且帧级PSNR>40dB、SSIM>0.996，攻击帧具有高度不可察觉性，同时现有篡改检测模型难以识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统依赖基于视频的感知系统支持安全关键应用，但攻击者可能操纵视频帧以破坏下游感知模块，导致安全关键功能失效并增加对弱势道路使用者的风险。目前缺乏针对视频目标移除攻击的端到端框架研究，需要探明此类攻击的可行性和威胁程度。

Method: 提出四阶段端到端攻击管线：(1)定位每帧中的目标；(2)从早期帧中检索连贯的补丁；(3)使用上下文感知的alpha合成进行混合；(4)重建被攻击的帧。实验在南卡罗来纳州互联车辆试验台（SC-CVT）的真实交叉路口场景进行。

Result: 重建帧与原始帧高度相似（PSNR>40dB，SSIM>0.996）；对YOLO检测器实现高达97.59%的检测率降低和94.48%的帧级攻击成功率；在GPU上平均每帧执行时间0.074-0.172秒，达到近实时性能；多个预训练篡改检测模型难以区分重建帧与真实帧。

Conclusion: 视频感知系统在对抗性目标移除攻击下具有脆弱性，此类隐蔽攻击可显著降低安全关键应用的目标可检测性。研究结果有助于制定缓解策略，以防御威胁安全关键应用（如基于视觉的行人安全系统）的对抗性目标移除攻击。

Abstract: By leveraging data from video-based perception systems, intelligent transportation systems (ITS) support safety-critical applications that improve road safety. However, adversaries may manipulate video frames to compromise downstream perception modules, causing failures in safety-critical functions and increasing risks to vulnerable road users. This paper presents a novel attack model and an end-to-end framework for near-real-time targeted object removal attack on a video-based safety-critical system. The end-to-end attack pipeline consists of four stages: localizing targets in each frame, retrieving coherent patches from earlier frames, blending them using context-aware alpha compositing, and reconstructing attacked frames. Experiments at an intersection on the South Carolina Connected Vehicle Testbed (SC-CVT) show that reconstructed frames have high global similarity to the originals, with frame-level Peak Signal to Noise Ratio (PSNR) above 40 dB and Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) above 0.996. Using the YOLO-based detector, the attack reduces object detections by up to 97.59% and achieves a frame-level attack success rate of 94.48%. Across the evaluated detectors and frame resolutions, the mean execution time ranges from 0.074 to 0.172 seconds per frame on GPU hardware, indicating near-real-time performance in testing. The forensic evaluation using several pretrained tamper-detection models shows limited ability to distinguish reconstructed from authentic frames. The findings suggest that video-based perception is vulnerable to stealthy object removal attacks that can degrade the performance of safety-critical applications by reducing object detectability. These findings can help develop mitigation strategies against adversarial object removal attacks that threaten safety-critical applications, such as vision-based pedestrian safety systems.

</details>


### [223] [Evading Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Through Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02820)
*Giorgio Severi,Shujaat Mirza,Blake Bullwinkel,Amanda Minnich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究思维链（CoT）监控的局限性，通过模型投毒视角证明了可以在推理模型中被植入后门（CoT-Hidden backdoors），使模型行为符合攻击者意图，但其思维链轨迹看起来完全正常。研究发现这些后门可通过简单的微调配方诱导，并引入了一种课程式训练方法使直接投毒失效时也能隐藏行为。因果干预表明触发条件激活通路不依赖可见推理，残差流言语化在答案生成附近提供了异常预警，但无法识别触发器、目标或后门机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是AI安全栈中越来越重要的组成部分，但其有效性建立在"模型的推理轨迹能反映其行为"这一假设之上。本文旨在探索这一假设的弱点，通过研究后门攻击能否在保持思维链轨迹表面正常的同时控制模型行为，从而揭示CoT监控的局限性。

Method: 研究采用了两种投毒方法：1）简单的微调配方，通过微调在推理模型中植入CoT-Hidden后门；2）当直接投毒无效时，引入课程式训练方法，逐步教导模型在隐藏推理痕迹的同时产生攻击者选择的输出。此外，采用因果干预方法定位触发条件激活通路，并通过残差流言语化（residual stream verbalization）来分析模型的内部机制。

Result: 实验结果表明，CoT-Hidden后门可以在多种推理模型架构和规模上通过简单微调被植入。因果干预定位到一条不依赖于可见推理的触发条件激活通路。残差流言语化在答案生成附近能提供异常预警，但无法识别触发器、攻击目标或后门的具体机制。

Conclusion: CoT监控应被重新定义为"模型推理轨迹与最终回答之间的一致性"问题，而非"轨迹内的异常检测"问题。现有CoT监控方法在面对精心设计的后门攻击时存在根本性局限，需要开发新的监控范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is an increasingly important component of AI safety stacks but relies on the assumption that a model's reasoning trace is informative about its actions. This work studies the limits of CoT monitoring through the lens of model poisoning. We demonstrate that backdoors can be implanted into reasoning models to elicit an attacker-chosen behavior while their CoT traces appear entirely benign. We find that these CoT-Hidden backdoors can be induced through simple fine-tuning recipes across reasoning-model architectures and sizes. When direct poisoning is ineffective, we introduce a curriculum training approach that progressively teaches the model to produce an attacker-chosen output while concealing the behavior from its reasoning traces. These findings suggest that CoT monitoring may be better framed as a question about the consistency between a model's reasoning trace and its final response than as anomaly detection within a trace. We further examine the mechanisms that allow models to suppress evidence of the target behavior from their reasoning traces. Causal interventions locate a trigger-conditioned activation pathway that does not depend on the visible reasoning, and residual stream verbalizations provide an anomaly warning near answer generation, but do not identify the trigger, target, or backdoor mechanism.

</details>


### [224] [What the Detector Can See: Evaluating CPS Anomaly Detectors Independently of the Decision Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02821)
*Peiran Shi,Jian Xiang,Xiang Zhang,Chenglong Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖报警阈值（decision-rule-free）的CPS异常检测器评估方法：将检测器视为"观测→残差→报警"两阶段流水线，仅用归一化残差能量直接评估Stage 1（残差生成），该方法与KL散度有精确联系，可分别度量攻击分离度、训练-测试稳定性及编码紧凑性。在SWaT、WADI、HAI三个基准上对五个检测器评估后发现，尽管ROC-AUC相近，其性能在相同虚警率下可差一个数量级，且排名随测试台变化，揭示检测失败源于表示薄弱、阈值标定差或攻击物理效应弱等不同原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测器（从深度神经网络到不变模板）常以单一工作点上的precision/recall/F1或ROC-AUC进行比较，但这些指标混叠了两个独立因素：检测器对物理过程的表示质量与报警阈值的设置好坏。这导致无法区分检测失败究竟源于表示能力不足还是阈值标定问题，也无法跨检测器进行公平比较。

Method: 将CPS异常检测器解耦为两阶段流水线：Stage 1将观测映射为残差，Stage 2将残差映射为报警。不评估最终报警，而是直接用归一化残差能量评估Stage 1，该量与训练正常参考分布的KL散度存在精确数学联系。该方法不依赖特定报警规则，可独立量化三个维度：攻击分离度、跨训练-测试差距的稳定性、以及检测器编码物理过程的紧凑性。无需任何逐检测器调参，将其应用于GDN、FuSAGNet、TranAD、NSIBF和GeCo五个检测器，在SWaT、WADI、HAI三个CPS基准上进行评估。

Result: 五个检测器在SWaT上ROC-AUC相近，但在相同虚警率下性能相差超过一个数量级。排名跨测试台不稳定：TranAD在HAI上排第一但在SWaT上排最后；NSIBF在WADI上第一但在HAI上垫底。在WADI上，局部化攻击可规避跨通道聚合证据的检测器，这解释了为何NSIBF（不如此聚合）优于在其他基准上表现良好的方法。

Conclusion: 检测失败可源于三类不同来源：表示能力薄弱、阈值标定不良、或攻击本身物理效应微弱。基于决策规则无关（不依赖报警阈值）的分析能够有效分离这些因果因素，为CPS异常检测器的公平评估和故障诊断提供了更精准的工具。

Abstract: Anomaly detectors are often the last line of defense for cyber-physical systems (CPS). But detectors built in very different ways, from deep neural networks to invariant templates, are usually compared using precision, recall, or F1 at a single operating point. These scores mix two separate things: how well the detector represents the physical process, and how well its alarm threshold is set. We therefore treat a CPS anomaly detector as a two-stage pipeline: Stage 1 maps observations to residuals, and Stage 2 maps residuals to alarms. Instead of scoring only the final alarms, we evaluate Stage 1 directly using normalized residual energy, which has an exact connection to the Kullback-Leibler divergence from the trained-normal reference distribution. Because it does not depend on a specific alarm rule, it can separately measure attack separation, stability across the train-test gap, and the compactness with which a detector encodes the plant.
  Without any per-detector tuning, we apply this evaluation to five detectors -- GDN, FuSAGNet, TranAD, NSIBF, and GeCo -- across three CPS benchmarks: SWaT, WADI, and HAI. Although the detectors have similar ROC-AUC values on SWaT, their performance differs by more than an order of magnitude at a common false-alarm rate. Rankings also change across testbeds: TranAD ranks first on HAI but last on SWaT, while NSIBF ranks first on WADI but last on HAI. On WADI, localized attacks can evade detectors that pool evidence across all channels, helping explain why NSIBF outperforms methods that do well on other benchmarks. These results show that detection failure can come from different sources: a weak representation, poor threshold calibration, or an attack with little physical effect. A decision-rule-free analysis helps separate these causes.

</details>


### [225] [Decidability of Parameterised Dolev-Yao Secrecy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02838)
*Ioana Boureanu,R. Ramanujam,Srinibas Swain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Dolev-Yao模型中参数化保密性（parameterised secrecy）的验证问题。在会话数量无界、作为参数的条件下，作者证明了在两种结构限制（每角色的全局有界新鲜性、限制为良型替换的Dolev-Yao攻击者）下，参数化保密性是可判定的，并通过切割定理（cut-off theorem）和良结构转移系统（WSTS）约化建立有限证据，为小实例分析的经验有效性提供了形式基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Dolev-Yao保密性只关心协议是否在任意执行次数下泄露秘密，而参数化保密性关注保密性是否在所有系统规模（参与者数量、会话数量）上一致成立。这更贴近实际攻击如何随参与者规模扩展，并为小规模实例分析在符号协议验证中的经验有效性提供理论基础。

Method: 论文识别两种使参数化保密性可判定的结构限制：(i) 每个角色具有全局有界的新鲜性（bounded freshness per role）；(ii) Dolev-Yao攻击者被限制为良型替换（well-typed substitutions）。在这些假设下，协议执行可通过对代理和项上的折叠映射（collapsing map）获得有限表示。论文进一步证明诱导的转移系统在界基序（bound-based ordering）下构成良结构转移系统（WSTS），从而将保密性约化为WSTS中的覆盖性问题（coverability problem）。

Result: 主要结果：在该设定下参数化保密性可判定。获得了切割定理：具有任意多会话的系统中保密性违反总能被有界大小的系统所验证。此外，由于转移系统构成WSTS，保密性约化为覆盖性问题，这从结构上解释了符号协议分析中有限证人存在的原因。

Conclusion: 参数化保密性在一般情形下（即使有界新鲜性或有限消息大小）不可判定，但通过两种结构限制可实现可判定性。该工作将Dolev-Yao协议验证与参数化验证技术（如WSTS）联系起来，为经验上有效的小实例分析提供了形式化的理论依据。

Abstract: We study the verification of parameterised secrecy for cryptographic protocols in the Dolev-Yao model, where the number of protocol sessions is unbounded and treated as a parameter. This differs fundamentally from classical Dolev-Yao secrecy, which asks whether a protocol leaks a secret irrespective of the number of executions; our question is whether secrecy holds uniformly across all system sizes, where such a size is a parameter. This parameterised perspective captures how attacks scale with the number of participants and provides a formal basis for the empirical effectiveness of small-instance analysis.
  Secrecy (parameterised or not) is undecidable in general, even under bounded freshness or bounded message size. We identify two structural restrictions that make parameterised secrecy decidable: (i) global bounded freshness per role, and (ii) a Dolev-Yao intruder restricted to well-typed substitutions. Under these assumptions, protocol executions admit a finite representation up to a collapsing map on agents and terms.
  Our main result is that parameterised secrecy is decidable in this setting. We obtain a cut-off theorem: secrecy violations in systems with arbitrarily many sessions are always witnessed in systems of bounded size. The cut-off is self-contained; more strongly, the induced transition system forms a well-structured transition system (WSTS) under a bound-based ordering, so secrecy also reduces to a coverability problem in WSTS. This provides a structural explanation for the existence of finite witnesses in symbolic protocol analysis and connects Dolev-Yao verification with parameterised verification techniques.

</details>


### [226] [MutMem: Cryptographically Authorized Mutation in Persistent Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02843)
*Walid Saidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MutMem，一个在HOM-AIMOS持久性智能体记忆引擎上的授权变异协议。它保留记忆内容，记录带签名的正负结果证据（无年龄期限），并将每次非平凡权重变更作为经管理员授权的转换提交。每个转换绑定终端溯源节点、签名者纪元、量化新旧权重、无分叉前驱及两个域分离的SHA-256承诺。Ed25519验证在数据库写入器和便携验证器中运行。疑似毒化内容以带签名的可修订标签保留，作为召回时的信任证据。实验表明HOM-AIMOS在LongMemEval上达到91.8%的准确率，在LoCoMo上达到74.12%的判定准确率，原生套件通过所有声明的授权、拓扑、篡改、签名纪元及变异后召回测试，中位签名转换延迟为4.865ms。在N=100的PoisonedRAG适应中，攻击顶5披露无注入毒化内容（0/100，95% Wilson上界3.70%），98个干净负目标中的诱导目标回答攻击成功率为1/98（1.02%）。预注册四臂消融将检索减少归因于签名存储标签：绕过认知策略时检索器为94/100个目标选择毒化内容，恢复标签后为0/100。MutMem提供完整性、授权、可追溯性和历史连续性证据，但不确立内容真理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性智能体记忆需要随后的结果改变先前证据而动态适应，但可变的检索权重产生归因问题：审查者必须区分授权适应与数据库篡改。现有记忆系统缺乏区分合法权重更新与恶意篡改的机制，因此需要一种授权变异协议来确保记忆变化的可信性和可追溯性。

Method: MutMem采用授权变异协议：1) 保留记忆内容，记录带签名的正负结果证据，不设置年龄性过期；2) 每次非平凡权重变更作为管理员授权的转换提交；3) 每个转换绑定终端溯源节点、签名者纪元、量化新旧权重、无分叉前驱及两个域分离的SHA-256承诺；4) 在数据库写入器和便携验证器中均运行Ed25519签名验证；5) 疑似毒化内容以带签名的可修订标签保留，作为召回时的信任证据。

Result: HOM-AIMOS在LongMemEval上以LLM判定答对459/500题（91.8%）；LoCoMo上获得74.12%判定准确率和58.20 token F1。原生套件通过所有声明的授权、拓扑、篡改、签名纪元及变异后召回测试，中位签名转换延迟4.865ms。在PoisonedRAG适应中，攻击顶5披露无毒化内容（0/100），诱导目标攻击成功率仅1.02%。四臂消融证实签名的存储标签对中毒检索抑制起关键作用。”

Conclusion: MutMem为持久性智能体记忆提供了完整性、授权、可追溯性和历史连续性的证据基础，能够有效区分授权适应与数据库篡改，并显著抑制毒化内容的检索泄露。但论文明确指出，该系统并不确立内容本身的真理性，即它保障的是记忆变更的可信流程而非内容的真实性。

Abstract: Persistent agent memory must adapt as later outcomes change earlier evidence, yet mutable retrieval weights create an attribution problem: reviewers must distinguish authorized adaptation from database tampering. We present MutMem, an authorized-mutation protocol in HOM-AIMOS, a persistent agent-memory engine.
