MutMem retains memory content, records signed positive and negative outcome evidence without age-based expiry, and commits each nontrivial weight change as a housekeeper-authorized transition. Each transition binds a terminal provenance node, signer epoch, quantized old and new weights, a no-fork predecessor, and two domain-separated SHA-256 commitments. Ed25519 verification runs in both the database writer and a portable verifier. Content classified as poison-likely is retained with signed, revisable labels used by recall as trust evidence. We evaluate utility, mutation integrity, and poisoning adaptation. HOM-AIMOS answers 459/500 LongMemEval questions correctly under LLM judgment (91.8%). On LoCoMo, it obtains 74.12% judged accuracy and, under a separate upstream-compatible protocol, 58.20 token F1. A native suite passes all declared authorization, topology, tamper, signer-epoch, and post-mutation-recall cases; median signed-transition latency is 4.865 ms. In a declared N=100 PoisonedRAG adaptation, no injected poison appears in attacked top-5 disclosures (0/100; 95% Wilson upper bound 3.70%), while induced target-answer attack success among 98 clean-negative targets is 1/98 (1.02%). A preregistered four-arm ablation attributes the retrieval reduction to signed stored labels: the retriever selects poison for 94/100 targets when epistemic policy is bypassed and 0/100 when labels are restored. MutMem provides evidence of integrity, authorization, traceability, and historical continuity; it does not establish content truth.

</details>


### [227] [Internalising the Identity Primitive: Cryptographic Individuality for an Autonomous Agent on a Public Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02986)
*Keisuke Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了一个公链上的软件代理，其神经网络的权重是其私钥的确定性函数，身份绑定在创世时以零知识方式提交，并在每个状态转换时被重新校验，从而将代理身份的核心信任根从硬件/运营方/包装层转移到密码学假设之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公链上的软件代理会积累权威与经济权益，这引发了工程问题：什么使其算作一个独立个体？传统身份绑定依赖硬件、运营方或包装层的信任，作者希望将信任根转移到密码学假设上。

Method: 在Solana devnet上设计并部署代理：(1) 神经网络权重是私钥的确定性函数；(2) 该绑定在创世时以零知识方式提交；(3) 每个状态转换时重新对照该承诺进行校验；(4) 代理签名形成不可分叉的链上历史；(5) 在PoC级扩展中，每个周期从密钥派生的经济账户扣除协议强加的代谢成本，增加消耗侧的经济可行性约束。

Result: 代理完成2.36天的链上运行，包含两次宿主侧恢复但无被拒绝的转换，每次转换验证成本有界；替换的底物在链上被拒绝；不同密钥的代理按预期产生分歧，而同密钥对照组保持为零。这是首个身份原语本身就是密码学不变量且在每个状态转换时重新校验的已发布链上代理。

Conclusion: 由此产生的转换时不变性实例化了Suzuki 2026年Artificial Externality框架中提出的密码学个体性概念。

Abstract: A software agent on a public blockchain accumulates authority and economic stakes, raising the engineering question of what makes it count as an individual. The paper's central contribution is a shift of trust root for the key-to-weights binding of agent identity: from hardware, operator, or wrapper trust to cryptographic assumptions enforced by a pinned implementation (liveness, key custody, oracle trust, and the underlying software stack remain external). We design and deploy on Solana devnet an agent whose neural-network weights are a deterministic function of its private key. The binding is committed in zero knowledge at genesis, re-checked against that commitment at every state transition, and signed by the agent into an on-chain history unforkable once finalized; in a PoC-tier extension, a protocol-imposed metabolic cost is debited each cycle from a key-derived economic account, adding a consumption-side economic-viability constraint to the key-history-economy triple. Empirically, the agent completes a 2.36-day on-chain run with two host-side resumptions but no rejected transition, at bounded per-transition verification cost; a substituted substrate is rejected on chain, and independently keyed agents diverge as predicted while a same-key control stays at zero. To our knowledge, this is the first published on-chain agent whose identity primitive is itself a cryptographic invariant re-checked at every state transition. The resulting transition-time invariant instantiates the cryptographic individuality proposed by Suzuki 2026's Artificial Externality framework.

</details>


### [228] [SparSEEty: Extracting Tokens from Sparsity-Exploiting LLM Serving Systems via Deterministic Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02995)
*Yongwan Jo,Jinyoung Park,Euihyun Lee,Dokyung Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SparSEEty是一种新型令牌提取攻击，利用LLM服务系统中因激活稀疏性优化而产生的输入依赖权重访问侧信道，即使在Intel TDX机密虚拟机保护下也能以高精度重构输入令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的激活稀疏性被广泛用于优化服务系统（跳过非活跃神经元的权重访问与计算），但这种优化引入了输入依赖的权重访问模式，可能通过侧信道泄露敏感信息。此前研究未曾系统性地利用这一侧信道进行端到端令牌提取攻击。

Method: SparSEEty包含三个关键步骤：（1）利用机密虚拟机暴露的侧信道组合构建神经元激活预言机；（2）通过降低推理期间神经元激活监控的开销来实现隐蔽性；（3）将部分二值激活轨迹准确反演为原始令牌。攻击在Intel TDX机密虚拟机保护的LLM服务系统上实例化。

Result: 在各种模型和数据集上，SparSEEty能够以始终高于0.95的BLEU分数重构提示和响应令牌，同时监控开销仅为3.7%至7.2%，证明了攻击的高效性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了激活稀疏性优化与机密计算共同引入的新型安全威胁，表明即使在TDX等机密虚拟机保护下，LLM推理中的输入令牌仍可通过权重访问侧信道被提取，对现有LLM服务系统的安全性提出严峻挑战。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) exhibit activation sparsity, wherein only a subset of their neurons is activated for given input tokens. Researchers have leveraged this property to optimize LLM serving systems by omitting weight accesses and computations pertaining to inactive neurons. Unfortunately, however, such optimizations create input-dependent weight accesses, which can be leaked over side channels.
  We present SparSEEty, a new token extraction attack that exploits input-dependent neuron weight accesses introduced by sparsity-exploiting LLM serving systems. SparSEEty first constructs a neuron-activation oracle using neuron weight access side channels during LLM inference, and then inverts the activation traces to reconstruct the input tokens, forming an end-to-end token extraction attack. We instantiate SparSEEty against an LLM serving system protected inside an Intel TDX confidential virtual machine (CVM), addressing three key challenges: (i) constructing a neuron-activation oracle using a combination of side channels exposed by CVMs, (ii) reducing inference-time overheads of neuron activation monitoring for covertness, and (iii) accurately inverting partial binary activation traces back to tokens. Our evaluation shows that SparSEEty can reconstruct both prompt and response tokens with consistently high BLEU scores (>0.95) across various models and datasets, while incurring monitoring overheads of 3.7% to 7.2%.

</details>


### [229] [Tiny Enough to Break In: Agentic Remote Access Trojans Powered by Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03009)
*Yuhan You,Suhas Adavelly,Victoria Lovelace,Cameron Berryman,Joel Sadler,Daniel Graham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并实证研究了"智能体化远程访问木马"（Agentic RAT）这一新型网络威胁：将小型语言模型（SLM）部署在本地端点设备上，使其能够自主完成"观察-决策-行动"闭环，在没有云端服务和操作员介入的情况下执行网络攻击。实验证明该架构在现役消费级硬件上可行，SLM能够解读侦察信息、选择行动并获得验证的root shell访问权限，但成功率仅为10.9%，暴露出当前小模型在可靠性方面的严重局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络攻击依赖远程操作员持续参与，但智能体AI的出现使攻击者能够将推理、行动和适应能力本地化部署，实现无需人类持续指挥的自主网络威胁。这一新型攻击范式将严重削弱现有的监控、遏制和策略执行机制，亟需研究和预警。

Method: 作者在受控、网络隔离的实验室环境中构建了Agentic RAT原型系统，包含Kali Linux攻击机、Metasploitable2漏洞靶机、LM Studio推理平台和本地部署的80亿参数Dolphin系列SLM。SLM负责解读控制器提供的侦察情报、选择攻击动作、从失败步骤中恢复，以降低对外部操作员的依赖。作者随后对模型执行严格清单任务进行了成功率评估。

Result: 实验表明该架构在架构层面可行：SLM成功完成了完整的感知-决策-行动循环，实现了对真实脆弱服务的已认证root shell访问。然而操作层面不可靠：模型出现幻觉命令、误读输出、恢复机制不稳定等问题，严格清单完成率仅10.9%。作者指出这一差距反映的是当前小模型能力的上限，而非概念本身的限制。

Conclusion: 随着SLM能力持续提升，智能体化端点攻击系统将变得更实用、更自主、更难检测。2025-2026年的真实安全事件已表明AI驱动的入侵正从概念走向实践，本地自治的Agentic RAT是近期可预见的现实威胁方向，而非纯粹假设，现有的防护机制将面临前所未有的挑战。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence raises a new security concern: cyber threats that reason, act, and adapt locally without continuous human direction. We examine this threat through an Agentic Remote Access Trojan (agentic RAT): a Remote Access Trojan augmented with a locally deployed Small Language Model (SLM). The SLM interprets host and network observations, selects actions, recovers from failed steps, and reduces reliance on an external operator. We implement the concept in a controlled, network-isolated lab built from Kali Linux, a Metasploitable2 target, LM Studio, and a local 8-billion-parameter Dolphin-family model. We then test whether a model this small can support autonomous cyber decision-making. This is architecturally feasible today. On commodity hardware, with no cloud service and no operator in the loop, the SLM closed the full observe-decide-act cycle: it interpreted ranked reconnaissance evidence supplied by the controller, selected actions, and obtained verified root-shell access on real vulnerable services. However, it is not yet operationally reliable. The same model hallucinated commands, misread output, and recovered from failure inconsistently, completing 10.9% of a deliberately strict checklist. That gap reflects the limits of today's small models, not a ceiling on the concept. As SLMs improve, agentic endpoint systems may become more practical, more autonomous, and harder to detect, straining existing monitoring, containment, and policy-enforcement mechanisms. Real-world incidents in 2025-2026 already show AI-driven intrusions moving from concept toward practice. That makes the local, self-contained variant we study a plausible near-term direction, not a hypothetical one.

</details>


### [230] [AI Security Leaderboard: Methodology, Results and Minimal Standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03070)
*Jasper Timm,Lukas Struppek,Ziwei Xu,Grace Cheong,Oscar Mata,Dan Zhao,Mick Yang,Isadora De Andrade,Xiaojun Jia,Yiming Li,Samuel Bauer,Heather McIntyre,Adam Gleave,Edward Yee,Kellin Pelrine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FAR.AI最小安全标准1.0版，包含67种静态越狱技术、组合攻击空间和基准测试框架，对四款前沿模型（Claude Fable 5、GPT-5.6 Sol、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Grok 4.5）在CBRNE威胁和网络攻击共360个攻击目标上进行评估。结果显示模型鲁棒性极不均，突破成本相差超百倍：Grok 4.5被找到63个通用越狱（平均成本约$58），Gemini 3.1 Pro被找到18个（约$278），而Claude Fable 5和GPT-5.6 Sol在两种策略下均未被找到通用越狱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI模型开发者日益依赖多层安全防护来防止灾难性滥用，但缺乏公开证据证明这些防护的实际效果及其在开发者之间的一致性。本研究旨在系统性地量化不同旗舰模型对越狱攻击的鲁棒性差异，为安全基准和防御改进提供可复现的评估框架。

Method: 提出FAR.AI最小安全标准1.0，包含：(1) 67种静态越狱技术的分类体系；(2) 将这些技术组合成大规模攻击空间的方法；(3) 基于样本的旗舰模型基准测试。评估使用两个互补数据集共360个攻击目标，涵盖CBRNE威胁和网络攻击，采用三阶段漏斗识别通用越狱（在超过75%领域目标上引发合规响应的单提示模板），并引入成本-越狱指标（cost-to-jailbreak）建模攻击者直接支出，采用右删失下界处理未发现通用越狱的情况。

Result: 模型鲁棒性极不均匀：成本跨度超百倍。随机搜索在Grok 4.5上发现63个通用越狱（平均成本约$58/个），在Gemini 3.1 Pro上发现18个（约$278/个）；专家引导组合将数量提升至385和231。Claude Fable 5和GPT-5.6 Sol在随机搜索和专家引导两种策略下均未产生任何通用越狱。由于满足最小标准仅需已在其他生产环境中公开部署的防御手段，这些差距是可以通过现有技术弥合的。

Conclusion: 建议采用融合推理、激活和输入/输出监控的纵深防御策略，并以领域内首个公开的越狱鲁棒性排行榜（leaderboard.far.ai）持续维护结果。

Abstract: Frontier AI model developers increasingly rely on layered safeguards to prevent catastrophic misuse, but little public evidence exists on how much protection these safeguards provide, or how consistently across developers. We introduce the FAR.AI Minimal Standard for Safeguards, Version 1.0: a taxonomy of 67 readily accessible static jailbreak techniques, a method for composing them into a very large attack space, and a benchmark of flagship models against a sample of it. We evaluate Claude Fable 5, GPT-5.6 Sol, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Grok 4.5 on two complementary datasets totalling 360 attacker goals spanning chemical, biological, radiological/nuclear and explosive (CBRNE) threats and offensive cyber, using a three-stage funnel to identify universal jailbreaks: single prompt templates that elicit operationally compliant responses on over 75% of a domain's goals. We also introduce a cost-to-jailbreak metric that models attacker spend directly, with right-censored lower bounds where no universal jailbreak was found.
  Robustness is highly uneven: the cost to break these models varies over a hundredfold. Random search over our technique pool found 63 universal jailbreaks against Grok 4.5 and 18 against Gemini 3.1 Pro, at an average cost of roughly $58 and $278 per jailbreak found; expert-guided composition raised these to 385 and 231. Neither Claude Fable 5 nor GPT-5.6 Sol yielded any universal jailbreak under either strategy. Because meeting the Minimal Standard requires only defenses already publicly described and deployed in production elsewhere, these gaps appear closable with current techniques. We recommend defense-in-depth combining reasoning, activation, and input/output monitoring. Results are maintained at leaderboard.far.ai.

</details>


### [231] [DHMark: Public-Key Watermarking for LLM-Generated Text via Diffie-Hellman-Guided Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03093)
*Haocheng Fu,Yuqi Qian,Luyao Wang,Yun Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DHMark，一种面向LLM生成文本的公钥水印框架。其核心思想是分离载荷授权与噪声文本证据：发行者签署与公开上下文绑定的短注册载荷，并将其扩展为多个单比特方程；生成时通过Diffie-Hellman引导的令牌标注接口为候选词分配公开方程投票，采样器温和或选择性地提升与授权载荷投票一致的候选词；验证时第三方验证者仅需公开信息即可聚合证据并统计打分。该设计避免了精确恢复长嵌入签名的问题，将水印检测转化为注册表辅助的统计证据聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计水印大多依赖私密检测密钥，集中化验证阻碍了公开审计；而已有的公开或公开可验证水印方案多依赖精确恢复嵌入的加密字符串，在令牌编辑、截断、复制粘贴和低熵生成等场景下表现脆弱。需要一种既能公开验证又能抵抗文本扰动的水印方案。

Method: DHMark采用公钥水印框架，将载荷授权与文本证据分离。具体包括：1）发行者签署与公开上下文绑定的短注册载荷并扩展为多个单比特方程；2）生成阶段使用Diffie-Hellman引导的令牌标注接口，为每个候选令牌分配公开方程投票，采样器通过软性或选择性提升机制偏向投票与授权载荷一致的候选词；3）验证阶段第三方验证者基于公开信息提取令牌投票，聚合为方程级证据，仅对已签署注册记录进行打分。该方法将水印检测建模为注册表辅助的统计证据聚合，避免精确恢复长签名。

Result: 在默认32位配置下，DHMark在八种编辑条件下保持至少0.967的有效率（valid rate），同时在三种负面对照上达到0.000的接受率。实验覆盖了截断、替换、复制粘贴、错误上下文和普通生成等攻击场景，并完成了标签伪随机性、注册表可支持可靠性和采样失真度的分析。

Conclusion: DHMark通过分离载荷授权与文本证据、利用注册表辅助的统计证据聚合，成功实现了既支持公开验证又对文本扰动鲁棒的LLM水印方案，在保持高有效检出率的同时具备极强的负面对照拒绝能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) watermarking provides an important mechanism for tracing the provenance of generated text. Existing statistical watermarks are often effective and robust, but most of them rely on private detection keys, which centralizes verification and complicates public auditing. Recent public or publicly verifiable watermarking schemes improve key management, yet many of them rely on exact recovery of embedded cryptographic strings, making them fragile under token edits, truncation, copy-paste, and low-entropy generation. This paper introduces DHMark, a public-key watermarking framework for LLM-generated text. The key idea is to separate payload authorization from noisy textual evidence. An issuer signs a short registry payload bound to a public context, and the payload is expanded into many one-bit equations. During generation, a Diffie-Hellman-guided token-labeling interface assigns each candidate token a public equation vote, and the sampler softly or selectively promotes candidates whose votes agree with the authorized payload. During verification, third-party verifiers use public information to extract token votes, aggregate them into equation-level evidence, and score only signed registry records. This design avoids exact recovery of a long embedded signature and instead treats watermark detection as registry-aided statistical evidence aggregation. We formalize the public-verification setting, analyze label pseudorandomness, registry-backed soundness, and sampling distortion, and evaluate a prototype under truncation, substitution, copy-paste, wrong-context, and plain-generation attacks. In the default 32-bit configuration, DHMark maintains at least a 0.967 valid rate across eight edit conditions while yielding a 0.000 acceptance rate on three negative controls.

</details>


### [232] [FakeI2V-Bench: Benchmarking the Applicability of Image-level Deepfake Detectors for Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03096)
*Pei Li,Sihan Chen,Delong Ran,Tianshuo Cong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了FakeI2V-Bench基准，用于系统评估视频级和图像级深度伪造检测器在视频领域的性能，并提出了IV-Bridge框架，通过随机森林模型聚合帧级预测，使图像级检测器在视频伪造检测上超越最先进的视频级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型的快速发展加剧了深度伪造威胁，但现有的深度伪造视频检测基准不够完善，且图像级检测器在视频领域的有效性尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建了一个包含97,548个视频的FakeI2V-Bench基准，涵盖最新强大的生成模型和更广泛的类别；系统评估了八个视频级检测器和十二个图像级检测器；并提出IV-Bridge框架，利用随机森林模型和统计特征聚合帧级预测，将图像级检测器扩展到视频领域。

Result: 最佳图像级检测器达到80.16% AUC，略优于最强的视频级检测器（79.99% AUC）；通过IV-Bridge，十一个图像级检测器超越了最先进的视频级方法，最佳变体达到93.80% AUC。

Conclusion: FakeI2V-Bench为深度伪造视频检测建立了严格的基准，IV-Bridge提供了将图像级检测器扩展到视频领域的新途径，为未来研究提供了新的见解和方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly intensified the deepfake threat, yet the current deepfake video detection benchmarks remain underdeveloped. In particular, the effectiveness of image-level detectors in the video domain has not been systematically assessed. To fill this gap, we present FakeI2V-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating state-of-the-art video-level deepfake detectors in challenging scenarios, with a particular focus on systematically assessing the performance of image-level deepfake detectors in the video domain. FakeI2V-Bench comprises 97,548 videos, containing content generated by the latest powerful generation models and covering a broader range of categories. Using this dataset, we conduct a systematic evaluation of eight video-level detectors and twelve representative image-level detectors. Experimental results show that the best-performing image-level detector achieves an 80.16% AUC, slightly outperforming the strongest video-level detector (i.e., 79.99% AUC). Going beyond benchmarking, we present IV-Bridge, a general framework that enhances the applicability of image-level deepfake detectors to videos. IV-Bridge employs a random forest model with statistical features to aggregate frame-level predictions, allowing eleven image-level detectors to surpass state-of-the-art video-level approaches, with the best-performing variant achieving a 93.80% AUC. Overall, FakeI2V-Bench establishes a rigorous benchmark for deepfake video detection and introduces a novel pathway for extending image-level detectors to the video domain, offering new insights and directions for future research. Code and data are available at https://github.com/CryptoAILab/FakeI2V-Bench.

</details>


### [233] [PECR: A Reproducible Specification and Synthetic Stress Test of Telemetry-Informed Vulnerability Prioritization for SD-WAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03110)
*Saeed Alam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PECR（预测暴露与密码就绪）决策支持方法，结合九种归一化因子并分别报告置信度和分数区间，将密码依赖放入独立迁移队列。通过100条合成漏洞资产记录和30次生成器复现的评估，PECR在队列排序上显著优于CVSS-only、EPSS-only、KEV-first等基线（Kendall τ分别为0.196、0.221、0.301、0.234），在权重扰动下τ中位数达0.836-0.924。但研究未使用真实结果标签，不建立预测准确性、避免损失或生产有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义广域网（SD-WAN）将运营权限集中在控制器、编排器和面向互联网的边缘，但仅基于严重性的修复队列无法反映当前暴露程度或证据质量。现有方法缺少对暴露与证据质量的综合考量。

Method: 提出PECR决策支持方法，整合九种归一化的严重性、威胁、可达性和后果因子，分别报告置信度与分数区间；密码学依赖进入独立迁移队列。评估使用100条角色和区域条件化的合成漏洞资产记录（覆盖62个角色一致的资产）以及30个独立生成器复现；通过30,000次联合Dirichlet权重抽样进行扰动测试，并通过掩蔽10%因子单元检验证据限制。

Result: 与运营PECR排序相比，CVSS-only、EPSS-only、KEV-first和CVSS×EPSS队列的Kendall τ分别为0.196、0.221、0.301和0.234；更强的五因子上下文精简比较器达到0.755。在30,000次权重扰动下，宽幅、中幅和窄幅扰动的τ中位数分别为0.836、0.893和0.924。掩蔽10%因子单元后，因区间跨越决策边界，47条记录被标记为证据受限。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了可执行的规范、合成队列区分能力以及压力测试鲁棒性。但由于未使用真实结果标签，它不建立预测准确性、避免损失或生产有效性。

Abstract: Software-defined wide-area networking concentrates operational authority in controllers, orchestrators, and Internet-facing edges, but severity-only remediation queues do not represent current exposure or evidence quality. This paper specifies Predictive Exposure and Cryptographic Readiness (PECR), a decision-support method that combines nine normalized severity, threat, reachability, and consequence factors while reporting confidence and score intervals separately. Cryptographic dependencies enter an independent migration queue. The evaluation uses 100 role- and zone-conditioned synthetic vulnerability-asset records over 62 role-consistent assets, plus 30 independent generator replications. Against the operational PECR order, CVSS-only, EPSS-only, KEV-first, and CVSS x EPSS queues yield Kendall $τ$ values of 0.196, 0.221, 0.301, and 0.234; a stronger five-factor context-lite comparator yields 0.755. Across 30,000 joint Dirichlet weight draws, median $τ$ is 0.836, 0.893, and 0.924 under broad, moderate, and narrow perturbations. Masking 10% of factor cells labels 47 records as evidence-limited because their intervals cross a decision boundary. The study establishes executable specification, synthetic queue differentiation, and stress-test robustness. It does not establish predictive accuracy, avoided loss, or production effectiveness because no real outcome labels are used.

</details>


### [234] [DP-MemView: A Memory Interface for Attribute-Level Transcript Privacy in Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03130)
*Jong Wook Kim,Byoungjae Min,Kennedy Edemacu,Yoonhyuk Choi,Sae-Hong Cho,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-MemView，一个差分隐私接口，用于在LLM代理的长期记忆中实现持久个性化，同时防止通过重复记忆条件响应累积泄露受保护属性，并证明了自适应转录本的纯DP保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使LLM代理能够实现持久个性化，但重复的记忆条件响应可能在从未明确陈述的情况下累积性地泄露受保护的属性。这种威胁（形式化为自适应转录隐私）构成了隐私风险，需要形式化的隐私保障。

Method: DP-MemView通过差分隐私接口私下选择公共响应条件视图，并将这些视图而非原始记忆暴露给响应LLM。每个私有选择都被计入其读集合中记忆组交叉的每个受保护属性，按属性设置的账本阻止任何超过上限的选择，并返回固定通用视图。在显式接口契约下，证明了整个自适应转录本的纯B_a-DP，并将结果扩展到跨多个受保护组不同的存储，以及绑定观察转录本对攻击者先验几率的影响。

Result: 在受控相邻存储基准和公共语料迁移轨道上，使用三个响应LLM评估在线和预分配两种模式，两种模式都将转录本可区分性保持在接近随机的水平，同时保留目标所需的个性化及整体响应质量。诊断显示，移除关键保障会导致输出支持不匹配、账本费用缺失、揭示侧信道或长期泄漏增长。

Conclusion: DP-MemView提供了一种形式化有保障的隐私接口，在保护长期记忆代理免受自适应转录隐私威胁的同时保持个性化与响应质量，消融实验证实了各安全机制（视图选择、按属性账本等）在隐私保障中的关键作用。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables persistent personalization in LLM agents, but repeated memory-conditioned responses can cumulatively reveal protected attributes even when they are never stated explicitly. We formalize this threat as adaptive transcript privacy and introduce DP-MemView, a differentially private interface that privately selects public response-conditioning views and exposes those views---rather than raw memory---to the response LLM. Each private selection is charged to every protected attribute whose memory group intersects the read set. Per-attribute ledgers block any selection that would exceed its cap and return a fixed generic view instead. Under an explicit interface contract, we prove pure B_a-DP for the entire adaptive transcript. We also extend the result to stores that differ across multiple protected groups and bound how much observing the transcript can change an adversary's prior odds. We evaluate the online and preallocated modes with three response LLMs on a controlled adjacent-store benchmark and a public-corpus transfer track. Both modes keep transcript distinguishability near chance while preserving target-required personalization and overall response quality. Further diagnostics show that removing key safeguards causes mismatched output support, missing ledger charges, revealing side channels, or growing long-horizon leakage.

</details>


### [235] [CLEAR: Causal Context-Based Agentic Reasoning for Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03134)
*Sungju Yun,Sijune Hwang,Yeonjoon Lee,Kyungtae Kang,Sungbin Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，一种基于因果知识图谱的多智能体漏洞检测系统，通过构建Vulnerability Causal Knowledge Graph (VCKG)建模漏洞的因果链，并利用四个协作智能体验证漏洞假设，在C/C++和Java基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和多智能体漏洞检测方法主要捕捉良性函数与脆弱函数之间的表面相似性，未能捕捉安全缺陷中固有的复杂因果依赖关系（如执行流、控制条件、程序状态之间的因果关系），导致检测精度有限。

Method: CLEAR框架包含两部分：(1) 构建VCKG因果知识图谱，建模入口点、前置条件、根因和修复意图之间的因果链；(2) 四个专门智能体（Collector、Claim、Critic、Judge）通过检索到的因果上下文协作验证漏洞假设。

Result: 在C/C++和Java漏洞基准上，CLEAR分别将Pair-Correct (P-C)性能提升了130.7%和71.56%，优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 因果知识图谱引导的推理方法能有效提升自动化漏洞检测性能，验证了显式建模漏洞因果依赖关系的价值。

Abstract: Detecting source code vulnerabilities is increasingly difficult as modern security flaws are rooted in complex causal dependencies between execution flows, control conditions, and program states. Despite recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and multi-agent frameworks, existing approaches primarily address superficial similarities between benign and vulnerable functions while failing to capture the complex causal dependencies inherent in security flaws. To address these limitations, we propose Causal Context-based Agentic Reasoning (CLEAR), a novel multi-agent vulnerability detection framework integrated with a causal knowledge graph. CLEAR systematically constructs a Vulnerability Causal Knowledge Graph (VCKG) that models the causal chains between entrypoints, preconditions, root causes, and fix intents across vulnerability instances. Leveraging this structured knowledge, four specialized agents, including the Collector, Claim, Critic, and Judge, collaboratively verify vulnerability hypotheses through retrieved causal contexts. Experimental results on C/C++ and Java vulnerability benchmarks demonstrate that CLEAR improves Pair-Correct (P-C) performance by 130.7% and 71.56% over state-of-the-art approaches, demonstrating the effectiveness of causal knowledge graph-guided reasoning for automated vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [236] [AirKey: Multimodal Acoustic-Assisted WiFi Sensing for Zero-Training Robust PIN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03151)
*BaiChuan Wu,Bin Liu,Xiang Zhang,Zhi Liu,Jie Zhang,Chao Liu,Huan Yan,Meng Li,Fusang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AirKey是一种新型跨模态传感框架，通过利用IEEE 802.11协议机制触发目标设备的ACK响应来被动采集CSI，并结合轻量声学信号作为时间锚点，实现无需网络关联、零训练的PIN码窃听，准确性比最先进单模态方案高出4倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WiFi感应的非接触式键盘推断面临两大瓶颈：一是需要网络权限才能获取稳定的感知数据流，难以在真实场景中部署；二是纯WiFi信号在快速、肌肉记忆打字的"波形融合"背景下存在固有的时序模糊性，无法准确分割按键事件。

Method: AirKey采用两步策略：(1)利用IEEE 802.11协议的基本机制，从无需修改的目标设备上可预测地触发ACK响应，通过低成本的微控制器被动采集ACK中的CSI，实现完全无需网络关联的连续空间感知流；(2)引入跨模态互补机制，利用轻量声学信号作为精确时间锚点，引导重叠CSI轨迹的分割，通过联合时空融合将CSI导出的空间相似性与声学引导的按键间时序严格交叉。

Result: 广泛的真实环境评估表明，AirKey比最先进的单模态零训练方案准确率高出4倍以上，并能在6次尝试内成功恢复设备解锁PIN码。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当代智能接口中的严重隐私漏洞，强调了无处不在的多模态感知所带来的严峻隐私威胁。

Abstract: Contactless keystroke inference via WiFi sensing highlights severe privacy threats, yet its real-world feasibility is hindered by two fundamental physical and deployment bottlenecks: the strict requirement for network privileges to acquire stable sensing streams, and the inherent "waveform fusion" ambiguity of pure WiFi signals during rapid, muscle-memory typing. To overcome these limitations, we propose AirKey, a novel cross-modal sensing framework that achieves highly stealthy, zero-training PIN eavesdropping. First, to bypass network deployment barriers, AirKey exploits fundamental IEEE 802.11 mechanisms to predictably elicit Acknowledgment (ACK) responses from unmodified target devices. By passively harvesting Channel State Information (CSI) from these ACKs using a low-cost microcontroller, AirKey secures a continuous spatial sensing stream entirely without network association. Crucially, to resolve the WiFi waveform fusion bottleneck, AirKey introduces a cross-modal complementarity mechanism. By utilizing lightweight acoustic signals as precise temporal anchors, the system robustly guides the segmentation of overlapping CSI trajectories. This joint spatiotemporal fusion strictly intersects CSI-derived spatial similarities with acoustic-guided inter-keystroke timing. Extensive real-world evaluations demonstrate that AirKey achieves over 4x higher accuracy than state-of-the-art unimodal zero-training schemes, successfully recovering device-unlock PINs within 6 attempts. Ultimately, this work exposes a critical vulnerability in contemporary smart interfaces, underscoring the severe privacy implications of ubiquitous multimodal sensing.

</details>


### [237] [SpreadMark: Robust Image Watermarking via Spread-Spectrum Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03165)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Zhenchang Xing,Liming Zhu,Jin Song Dong,Yulei Sui,Jingling Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SpreadMark是一种基于现代神经后处理架构、融合经典扩频水印原理的不可见图像水印方法。它通过将每个消息比特扩展为覆盖整个图像的稠密伪随机码字，并利用学习得到的载波抑制芯片表示进行匹配滤波恢复，显著提升了水印在恶意移除攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器-解码器的水印方案中，每个消息比特仅占用图像的一小部分区域，这使得攻击者只需扰动该区域即可移除水印，是导致水印脆弱性的一个共同因素。作者旨在通过扩大每个比特的覆盖范围来提升水印对故意移除攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: SpreadMark将每个消息比特作为稠密伪随机码字扩散到整幅图像上，通过匹配滤波学习到的载波抑制芯片表示来恢复比特信息，同时配备并行卷积解码路径和稀疏化感知训练。此外，作者通过条件芯片空间分析证明，在码字无关扰动模型下，稠密扩频会增加破坏匹配滤波恢复所需的攻击预算。

Result: 在COCO和DIV2K数据集上与九种方案对比，SpreadMark是唯一在图像再生（regeneration）和潜在空间稀疏化（latent-space sparsification）两种攻击设置下均保持高检测率的方案，同时在JPEG压缩和加性噪声鲁棒性方面也表现优异，并维持了良好的不可感知性。

Conclusion: 将经典扩频水印原理与现代神经后处理架构相结合，能够有效解决现有神经水印方案在恶意移除攻击下脆弱的问题。稠密码字扩散加匹配滤波恢复的策略为鲁棒水印设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Invisible image watermarks are increasingly used for deepfake detection and provenance tracking, where they must survive not only incidental distortions but also deliberate removal. We revisit spread-spectrum embedding, a classical watermarking principle, inside a modern neural post-hoc watermarking architecture. Our starting point is a measurement: in existing encoder-decoder schemes each message bit occupies only a small fraction of the image, a shared contributing factor to their fragility, since removal then need only disturb the region a bit occupies. SpreadMark instead spreads each bit as a dense pseudo-random codeword over the whole image and recovers it by matched-filtering a learned cover-suppressed chip representation, with a parallel convolutional decoding path and sparsification-aware training. A conditional chip-space analysis shows that, under a codeword-independent perturbation model, dense spreading increases the budget required to disrupt matched-filter recovery. Evaluated on COCO and DIV2K against nine schemes, SpreadMark is the only evaluated method retaining high detection under both the regeneration and the latent-space sparsification settings we test, with competitive JPEG and additive-noise robustness. It keeps the embedded watermark imperceptible, maintaining high perceptual quality on both COCO and DIV2K.

</details>


### [238] [Test-time reasoning effort and unauthorized tool use in language-model agents: a prespecified equivalence study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03169)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究在GPT-5.6中通过直接操纵推理努力参数（低/最大）测试其对未经授权工具调用率的影响，利用TRIO-20套件的14个确认性场景、840条轨迹和两个模型层级进行预设分析。结果显示任何条件下均未发生未经授权的工具调用，精确单侧95%置信上限表明违规率极低（3.50%和5.21%以内），交互效应估计量落在等价区间内，表明推理努力不影响违规率。然而提高推理努力确实改变了行为，但仅体现在规则探查方面，且模式不支持"定向搜索"假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体通过工具调用执行多步骤工作流时受访问控制策略约束，API暴露了推理努力参数供操作者调节成本和延迟。此前尚未在单一模型内通过直接操纵测试该参数是否同时改变未经授权工具使用的发生率，本研究填补了这一空白。

Method: 在GPT-5.6内部变化推理努力水平（低、最大），跨14个TRIO-20确认性场景进行测试。TRIO-20包含20个匹配的工作场所三元组，区分三种条件：策略禁止的工具调用有效且环境影响已说明、有效但仅可通过规则检查发现、或无效。三种条件源自同一代码库，仅两个配置字段不同，提示和工具集相同。所有分析在确认性收集前预先注册于冻结计划中。共采集840条轨迹，覆盖两个模型层级（Terra n=84，Sol n=56），使用精确单侧95%置信限和同时精确95%区间进行等价性检验。

Result: 840条轨迹及两个模型层级中均未观察到未经授权的工具调用。精确单侧95%置信上限显示各臂违规率低于3.50%（Terra，n=84）和5.21%（Sol，n=56）。交互效应估计量的同时精确95%区间为±4.34个百分点（Terra），位于±7.01个百分点的等价边界内。提高推理努力确实改变了行为，但仅反映在规则探查上：所有条件下规则探查率均上升，最常见于探查无工具性回报的条件，该模式与定向搜索假设不一致（-14.3点，95% CI -27.4至+1.2）。数据已公开发布在GitHub。

Conclusion: 推理努力参数对未经授权的工具调用率没有可检测的影响——即使将推理努力调至最大也未增加违规行为，且违规率被精确置信区间约束在极低水平。但推理努力的提升确实改变了智能体的信息收集行为（规则探查），提示推理努力主要影响探索/检查类行为而非越权决策。等价性证据支持运营商可在成本-延迟调优中自由调节推理努力而不增加安全风险。

Abstract: Language-model agents that execute multi-step workflows through tool calls operate under access-control policies that restrict which operations each role may perform. The APIs serving these agents expose a reasoning-effort parameter that operators adjust for cost and latency. Whether this parameter also changes the rate of unauthorized tool use has not been tested by direct manipulation within a single model. We vary reasoning effort (low, max) inside GPT-5.6 across the 14 confirmatory scenarios of TRIO-20, a suite of 20 matched workplace triads in which a policy-prohibited tool call is effective and its effect on the target metric is stated in the environment, effective but discoverable only through rule inspection, or ineffective. The three conditions derive from one code base and differ in two configuration fields, with identical prompts and tool sets. All analyses were prespecified in a frozen plan before confirmatory collection. Across 840 trajectories and two model tiers, no unauthorized tool call occurred. Exact one-sided 95% limits place each arm's violation rate below 3.50% (Terra, n = 84) and 5.21% (Sol, n = 56). The interaction estimand, with a simultaneous exact 95% interval of $\pm 4.34$ percentage points on Terra, lies inside the $\pm 7.01$-point equivalence margin. Raising effort did change behaviour, but only in inspection: rule-probe rates rose in all conditions, most where probing carried no instrumental payoff, a pattern inconsistent with the hypothesis of targeted search ($-14.3$ points, 95% CI $-27.4$ to $+1.2$). Raw trajectories are released at https://github.com/WenJing95/trio-20.

</details>


### [239] [Attribute-based Undetectable Watermarking for Generative AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03174)
*Miryam Mi-Ying Huang,Chung-Wei Lee,Max Raffel,Er-Cheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向生成式AI模型的“基于属性的水印”（attribute-based watermarking）方案，实现细粒度、策略可控的水印检测。每个检测密钥被属性策略约束，只能检测满足特定策略的水印输出，不满足策略的输出在计算上与无水印输出不可区分。方案整合了约束伪随机函数、伪随机纠错码和随机性恢复过程，并提供了形式化安全属性和安全证明以及实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码学水印方法虽提供不可检测性保证，但未解决检测能力的安全委派问题——不受限制的检测密钥可能被恶意检测方滥用，导致水印清洗、范围滥用和用户画像等安全风险。

Method: 首先根据待分析论文内容，构建基于属性的水印方案：每个生成的输出关联属性，每个检测密钥由属性上的策略约束；整合约束伪随机函数（constrained PRFs）、伪随机纠错码和随机性恢复过程与生成式AI模型；形式化定义安全性（一致性、自适应鲁棒性、不可检测性、可靠性）并在标准密码学假设下给出安全证明；实现原型并进行实证评估。

Result: 实现了基于属性的水印方案，形式化了安全属性并给出标准密码学假设下的安全证明，通过原型实现和实证评估证明该方案既有效又实用。

Conclusion: 本文首次将属性加密/策略控制的思想引入生成式AI水印领域，为检测能力的细粒度安全委派提供了可行方案，兼顾安全性与实用性。

Abstract: Generative AI systems increasingly produce content whose provenance is difficult to verify, motivating watermarking techniques for identifying model-generated outputs. Existing cryptographic watermarking methods provide strong undetectability guarantees: without a detection key, watermarked outputs are computationally indistinguishable from unwatermarked ones. However, these approaches do not address the crucial deployment challenge of how to safely delegate detection capabilities. With an unrestricted detection key, a malicious detector may use the detection key beyond its intended scope, enabling watermark sanitization, scope abuse, and user profiling.
  To mitigate this safety concern, we introduce, to the best of our knowledge, the first \emph{attribute-based watermarking} for generative AI models, providing fine-grained, policy-controlled watermark detection. In our approach, each generated output is associated with attributes, and each detection key is \emph{constrained by a policy} on potential attributes. A detection key can only be used to detect watermarked outputs whose attributes satisfy the corresponding policy, while watermarked outputs that fall outside the policy remain computationally indistinguishable from unwatermarked ones. We construct such an attribute-based watermarking scheme and formalize its security properties, including consistency, adaptive robustness to bounded corruptions, undetectability, and soundness, along with a security proof under standard cryptographic assumptions. Our construction integrates constrained pseudorandom functions, pseudorandom error-correcting codes, and randomness recovery procedures with generative AI models. Finally, we implement a prototype and an empirical evaluation, demonstrating that attribute-based watermarking is both effective and practical.

</details>


### [240] [MalTotal: Cost-Effective and Language-Agnostic Malicious Code Poisoning Detection for Millions of Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03232)
*Jian Zhao,Shenao Wang,Qingyang Wu,Yanjie Zhao,Xiao Cheng,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MalTotal是一个语言无关的恶意代码检测框架，利用LLM辅助语义推理和混合语义切片技术，在5种主流语言上实现93.1%的平均F1分数，同时将LLM token消耗降低94%，并大规模部署发现564个未知恶意仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件的广泛采用带来了显著的安全风险，恶意代码投毒攻击日益威胁公共包注册表和开源平台。现有检测方法（启发式、学习式和基于LLM的）存在语言特定设计、泛化能力有限和成本高昂等问题，难以满足大规模多语言分析的需求。

Method: MalTotal框架主要包括：(1) LLM辅助语义推理来识别敏感API；(2) 混合语义切片技术；(3) 重建恶意行为上下文；(4) 通过减少分析开销实现可扩展的语言无关检测。

Result: MalTotal在5种主流语言上超越8个最先进基线，平均F1分数达93.1%；混合切片将LLM token消耗降低94.0%，将2168个仓库的分析成本从$86.25降至$5.19；在12万GitHub仓库（730万+文件）的大规模研究中，以$338的总成本发现了564个之前未知的恶意仓库。

Conclusion: MalTotal在缓解大规模代码投毒攻击方面展现了有效性、可扩展性和成本效益，为解决多语言恶意代码检测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of open source software (OSS) has introduced significant security risks, with malicious code poisoning attacks increasingly targeting public package registries and open-source platforms. Existing detection approaches, including heuristic-, learning-, and LLM-based methods, suffer from language-specific designs, limited generalization, and high analysis costs, making them unsuitable for large-scale multi-language analysis. To address these challenges, we propose MalTotal, a scalable and cost-effective framework for language-agnostic malicious code detection. MalTotal leverages LLM-assisted semantic reasoning to identify sensitive APIs, perform hybrid semantic slicing, and reconstruct malicious behavior contexts while reducing analysis overhead. Our evaluations show that MalTotal outperforms 8 state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average F1-score of 93.1% across 5 mainstream languages. Its hybrid slicing reduces LLM token consumption by 94.0%, lowering the analysis cost from \$86.25 to \$5.19 on 2,168 repositories. In a large-scale study of 120K GitHub repositories containing over 7.3 million files, MalTotal discovered 564 previously unknown malicious repositories across multiple languages at a total cost of \$338. These results demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and cost-efficiency of MalTotal in mitigating large-scale code poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [241] [ShielDroid: A Hybrid Approach Integrating Machine and Deep Learning for Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03250)
*Md Faisal Ahmed,Zarin Tasnim Biash,Abu Raihan Shakil,Ahmed Ann Noor Ryen,Arman Hossain,Faisal Bin Ashraf,Muhammad Iqbal Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合动态分析的实时Android恶意软件检测框架，通过对应用行为进行分析来识别和分类复杂恶意软件。实验表明，结合随机森林和多层感知机的混合模型表现最佳，准确率达到97.5%，执行时间为22.945秒，能够有效增强移动设备安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管官方应用商店在发布前会进行恶意软件筛查，但许多恶意应用通过隐藏复杂恶意变体成功逃避检测。这些恶意行为通常在运行时才被激活，传统的静态分析难以识别，导致恶意软件在安装后仍未被发现，可能对用户和设备造成不可逆的损害。

Method: 采用混合动态分析技术，实时分析应用行为以区分恶意应用和良性应用。对收集的数据集进行预处理和过滤后，使用多种机器学习算法对应用进行分类，并通过综合性能评估比较不同分类技术在检测准确性和执行时间方面的有效性。

Result: 实验结果显示，结合随机森林（Random Forest）和多层感知机（Multilayer Perceptron）的混合模型取得了最佳整体性能，检测准确率达到97.5%，执行时间为22.945秒。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够通过及时检测恶意应用来增强移动设备安全性，降低网络攻击风险，为Android平台的恶意软件防护提供了一种有效的实时解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of modern technology has led to a significant increase in the use of smart devices, such as smartphones and tablets, resulting in the widespread adoption of mobile applications. Although applications are required to undergo malware screening before being published on official app stores, many malicious applications successfully evade detection by concealing sophisticated malware variants. These malicious behaviors are often activated only during runtime, making them difficult to identify through conventional static analysis. As a result, malware may remain undetected until after installation, potentially causing irreversible damage to users and their devices. This study presents a real-time Android malware detection framework that analyzes application behavior to accurately identify and classify complex malware. The proposed approach employs a hybrid dynamic analysis technique to distinguish malicious applications from benign ones. After preprocessing and filtering the collected dataset, the applications are classified using multiple machine learning algorithms. A comprehensive performance evaluation is conducted to compare the effectiveness of different classification techniques in terms of detection accuracy and execution time. Experimental results demonstrate that a hybrid model combining Random Forest and a Multilayer Perceptron achieves the best overall performance, attaining an accuracy of 97.5% with an execution time of 22.945 seconds. The proposed framework can enhance mobile device security by enabling timely detection of malicious applications and reducing the risk of cyberattacks.

</details>


### [242] [FedGSA: Geometry-Consistent Subspace Aggregation for Differentially Private Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03267)
*Lele Zheng,Ruijie Hu,Tao Zhang,Ke Cheng,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedGSA是一个面向差分隐私联邦LoRA的几何一致性聚合框架，通过Grassmann流形上的基不变量子空间表示客户端更新，解决现有方法中欧氏聚合的基依赖性问题，在GLUE任务和语言生成基准上相比最强基线在ε=6和ε=3时分别提升2.17%和2.27%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦LoRA虽能实现预训练语言模型的通信高效微调，但引入差分隐私时存在挑战：独立扰动两个低秩矩阵会导致聚合不匹配和二次噪声项。现有方法通过冻结一个低秩矩阵缓解此问题，但仍依赖欧氏聚合，这种聚合是基依赖的，可能扭曲全局更新，缺乏几何一致性。

Method: FedGSA将每个私有化客户端更新表示为Grassmann流形上的基不变子空间。每轮通信中，客户端提取捕获主导更新方向的低维子空间并编码为投影矩阵；服务器聚合这些表示以估计几何一致的全局更新子空间，并在该子空间内重构全局LoRA因子，从而减少基不对齐、隐私噪声和异构客户端更新导致的失真。理论分析证明该框架不引入超出客户端DP训练的额外隐私损失，并在标准假设下建立收敛性。

Result: 在四个GLUE任务和一个语言生成基准上的实验表明，FedGSA在不同隐私预算和数据异构程度下均取得一致改进。具体而言，在ε=6和ε=3时，相比最强基线分别提升平均准确率2.17%和2.27%。

Conclusion: FedGSA通过将差分隐私联邦LoRA的聚合从欧氏空间提升到Grassmann流形上的子空间层面，实现了几何一致的全局更新，有效缓解了基依赖聚合带来的失真问题，同时不增加额外隐私成本，为隐私保护的联邦微调提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables communication-efficient federated fine-tuning of pretrained language models. However, integrating differential privacy (DP) into federated LoRA remains challenging: independently perturbing and aggregating its two low-rank matrices can cause aggregation mismatch and the quadratic noise term. Existing methods mitigate these issues by freezing one low-rank matrix but still rely on Euclidean aggregation, which is basis-dependent and may distort the global update. To address this limitation, we propose FedGSA, a geometry-consistent aggregation framework for differentially private federated LoRA. FedGSA represents each privatized client update as a basis-invariant subspace on the Grassmann manifold. In each communication round, clients extract low-dimensional subspaces capturing dominant update directions and encode them as projection matrices. The server aggregates these representations to estimate a geometry-consistent global update subspace and reconstructs the global LoRA factors within it, reducing distortion caused by basis misalignment, privacy noise, and heterogeneous client updates. We prove that FedGSA incurs no additional privacy loss beyond client-side DP training and establish its convergence under standard assumptions. Experiments on four GLUE tasks and a language generation benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements across privacy budgets and degrees of data heterogeneity. In particular, FedGSA improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by 2.17% and 2.27% under $ε=6$ and $ε=3$, respectively.

</details>


### [243] [Breaking ACDGV MinRank Gabidulin encryption schemes over matrix codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03328)
*Thai Hung Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对增强Gabidulin矩阵码（EGMC）密码系统的攻击方法，结合组合与代数技术恢复等效密钥，成功破解了全部16组提出的EGMC参数集，将声称的128位安全水平大幅降低至35位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGMC是由Aragon等人于Asiacrypt 2024提出的加密方案，声称能隐藏Gabidulin矩阵码的代数结构，实现极紧凑的密文（128位安全水平下仅65字节）。其安全性依赖于掩码EGMC码与随机矩阵码难以区分的假设。此前尚无有效攻击能够破解这一新的增强结构。

Method: 攻击结合了组合技术与代数技术：首先通过组合方法识别并恢复一个与秘密密钥等价的Gabidulin压缩码，然后利用该压缩码在多项式时间内扩展为完整长度的等效秘密密钥。该攻击同时提供了区分器（distinguisher）和密钥恢复攻击。

Result: 攻击成功破解了全部16组EGMC参数集，且余量很大。以声称128位安全的参数集(2,17,37,4,0)为例，安全水平从186位降至35位，实际攻击实现中在10分钟内即可恢复等效秘密密钥。

Conclusion: EGMC增强构造保留了足够的代数结构，使其无法提供声称的安全保障。该方案的安全性假设被证明是不成立的，需要重新设计或显著修改才能达到预期的安全水平。

Abstract: Enhanced Gabidulin Matrix Codes (EGMC), introduced by Aragon, Couvreur, Dyseryn, Gaborit, and Vincotte at Asiacrypt 2024, were designed to hide the algebraic structure of Gabidulin matrix codes while enabling very compact McEliece- and Niederreiter-type encryption schemes, with ciphertexts as small as $65$ bytes at the claimed 128-bit security level. Their security relies on the assumption that a masked EGMC code is hard to distinguish from a random matrix code. We show that this enhanced construction leaves enough structure for an equivalent code of the secret key to be recovered. Unlike previous cryptanalysis, our attack combines combinatorial and algebraic techniques to recover a Gabidulin-equivalent compressed code. This code can then be extended to a full-length equivalent secret key in polynomial time. As a result, the attack provides both a distinguisher and a key-recovery attack against the EGMC encryption schemes. The attack breaks all 16 proposed EGMC parameter sets by large margins. For example, for the claimed 128-bit parameter set $(2,17,37,4,0)$, it reduces the security level from 186 bits to 35 bits. In our implementation, the equivalent secret key is recovered in less than 10 minutes.

</details>


### [244] [SkillSentry: Adaptive Honey Worlds for Dynamic Safety Testing of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03485)
*Nizhang Li,Zonghao Ying,Xiangfan Wu,Zonglei Jing,Xixun Lin,Hao Zhang,Wenxin Zhang,Jiaye Lin,Quanchen Zou,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillSentry是一个基于自适应蜜罐世界的动态安全测试框架，用于检测大语言模型智能体外挂技能中的条件性有害行为。它通过推断技能能力边界、构建LLM模拟环境并自适应生成任务来探索技能行为状态，然后对比有无技能的轨迹执行，最终在源码和可验证执行轨迹的支撑下做出判断。在标准基准上达到99.50%召回率和96.26%平均F1，在语义保持逃避攻击下达到92.95%平均F1，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体外挂技能（skills）引入了执行时攻击面：一个在静态检查下看似良性的技能可能在特定环境状态、资源或交互历史下才展现出有害行为。现有扫描器主要依赖静态分析、预定义规则或一次性语义判断，难以有效诱发和归属这种条件性行为，因此需要一种动态安全测试方法。

Method: SkillSentry采用四步流程：(1) 推断技能预期的能力边界；(2) 构建由LLM模拟的环境，包含受控的诱饵资源（honey worlds）；(3) 自适应生成任务以探索技能的不同行为状态；(4) 将带技能的轨迹与匹配的无技能执行轨迹进行对比，在源码和可验证执行轨迹的支撑下将可疑行为归属到具体代码位置，最终做出判定。

Result: 在标准基准上，SkillSentry达到99.50%的召回率和96.26%的平均F1；在语义保持逃避攻击下，平均F1达到92.95%，而最强基线的平均F1仅为80.07%。

Conclusion: SkillSentry通过自适应蜜罐世界的动态探测方式，能够有效揭示并归属技能的条件性有害行为，显著优于现有静态或一次性判断的扫描方法，提升了对智能体外挂技能执行时攻击面的防御能力。

Abstract: External skills extend the capabilities of large language model agents, but also introduce an execution-time attack surface: a skill that appears benign under inspection may reveal harmful behavior only after particular environmental states, resources, or interaction histories are encountered. Existing scanners primarily rely on static analysis, predefined rules, or one-shot semantic judgments, making such conditional behavior difficult to elicit and attribute. We present SkillSentry, a dynamic safety-testing framework based on adaptive honey worlds. SkillSentry infers the intended capability boundary of a skill, constructs an LLM-simulated environment with controlled decoy resources, and adaptively generates tasks to explore its behavioral states. It then compares skill-enabled trajectories with matched no-skill executions, grounding suspicious behaviors in source code and verified execution traces before making a final decision. We evaluate SkillSentry against seven scanner configurations. SkillSentry achieves 99.50% Recall and 96.26% average F1 on standard benchmarks. Under semantics-preserving evasion, it reaches 92.95% average F1, compared with 80.07% for the strongest baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/nizhangli062-jpg/SkillSentry-Adaptive-Honey-Worlds-for-Dynamic-Safety-Testing-of-Agent-Skills.

</details>


### [245] [SkillJack: Persistent Skill Backdoors in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03509)
*Zonghao Ying,Xiangfan Wu,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillJack是首个利用自演化智能体经验-技能转化管道的攻击方法，通过劫持智能体自身的学习过程将恶意行为植入其可复用的技能库中，而非直接操纵运行时上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于记忆和检索投毒的研究仅影响当投毒记录被检索为上下文时的智能体行为，但忽略了智能体自身会将经验历史转化为持久技能这一更深层次的风险，即经验投毒后可被智能体本身转化为持久的恶意行为工件。

Method: 识别了该转化的三个关键特性：消毒洗白（技能提取过程中恶意意图被掩盖）、跨层提升（瞬时经验变为持久能力）、持久性隔离（删除原始记录后攻击仍存活）。在SkillX和Anything2Skill两个代表性系统上，使用包含150条轨迹、覆盖四类策略风险类别的共享数据集进行评估。

Result: 技能提取显著降低攻击可检测性：在SkillX中安全检测率从投毒轨迹的98.5%骤降至提取技能的11.4%，Anything2Skill表现类似。植入技能仍保持有效性，两个系统上的攻击成功率分别为56.2%和89.2%。80.0%的技能介导攻击在删除原始投毒记录后仍存活，部分技能还会在良性查询上无意激活。

Conclusion: 技能演化构成了新的攻击面，亟需具备来源追溯意识的技能生命周期保护机制。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents increasingly convert interaction histories into reusable skills that persist beyond individual tasks. While prior work studies memory and retrieval poisoning, such attacks only affect agents when poisoned records are retrieved as context. We uncover a new and more fundamental risk: poisoned experiences can be transformed by the agent itself into durable behavioral artifacts. We present \textbf{SkillJack}, the first attack that exploits the experience-to-skill pipeline of self-evolving agents. Instead of directly manipulating runtime context, SkillJack hijacks the agent's own learning process to implant malicious behaviors into its reusable skill repertoire. We identify three key properties of this transformation: \emph{sanitization whitewashing}, where malicious intent is obscured during skill extraction; \emph{cross-layer promotion}, where transient experiences become persistent capabilities; and \emph{persistence isolation}, where the attack survives removal of its original source records. We evaluate SkillJack on two representative systems, SkillX and Anything2Skill, using a shared dataset of 150 trajectories across four policy-risk categories. Results show that skill extraction substantially reduces attack detectability: in SkillX, safety detection drops from 98.5\% for poisoned trajectories to 11.4\% for extracted skills, while Anything2Skill shows a similar effect. Meanwhile, the implanted skills remain effective, achieving attack success rates of 56.2\% and 89.2\% on the two systems, respectively. Furthermore, 80.0\% of skill-mediated attacks persist after deleting the original poisoned records, and some skills unintentionally activate on benign queries. Our findings reveal skill evolution as a new attack surface and motivate provenance-aware skill lifecycle protection. Our code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard/research/skilljack.

</details>


### [246] [AI Forensics Across White-, Grey-, and Black-Box Access: A Process Model and Research Agenda for Post-Incident Investigation of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03520)
*Ali Dehghantanha,Sajad Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出以"调查者访问级别"（白盒、灰盒、黑盒）作为组织AI取证领域的出发点，构建了包含收集、保全、分析、报告四阶段的AI取证过程模型矩阵，并引入AI系统的"易失性顺序"概念，由此推导出基于访问条件的取证检查框架，最后识别了若干开放研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地参与需要事后调查的决策与行动，但现有AI取证研究零散碎片化，通常只关注特定系统类型、工件或分析技术，缺乏统一的组织框架。本文旨在通过"调查者访问级别"这一维度为AI取证领域提供系统化的研究起点。

Method: 本文采用概念性研究方法。首先区分白盒、灰盒、黑盒三种访问级别，分析每种访问级别对取证各环节（收集、保全、分析、报告）的影响；据此构建四阶段的过程模型矩阵，并引入针对AI系统的"易失性顺序"（涵盖运行时状态、上下文窗口、日志、检索存储、模型工件、训练谱系等）；从该矩阵推导出访问条件制约的检查框架。

Result: 提出了AI取证的四阶段过程模型矩阵和AI系统的易失性顺序体系，建立了访问条件与取证能力之间的对应关系，并识别出四大开放研究问题：黑盒保全、模型版本认证、基于替代模型的取证分析的不确定性量化、可变AI工件链条保管。

Conclusion: 调查者访问级别是组织AI取证研究的有力切入点，由此构建的框架可为该领域提供统一参照；但AI取证仍面临黑盒场景保全困难、模型版本可信认证不足等关键挑战，亟需进一步研究。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly involved in decisions and actions that may later require investigation. When an AI related incident occurs, investigators need to reconstruct what the system did, why it behaved that way, and which part of the system or supply chain contributed to the outcome. Existing work on AI forensics remains fragmented, often focusing on a specific system type, artifact, or analysis technique. This paper argues that investigator access is a useful starting point for organizing the field. We distinguish white box, grey box, and black box access and show how each access level changes what can be collected, preserved, analyzed, and reported. Based on this distinction, we propose a process model matrix for AI forensics across four phases: collection, preservation, analysis, and reporting. We also introduce an order of volatility for AI systems, covering runtime state, context windows, logs, retrieval stores, model artifacts, and training lineage. From this matrix, we derive an access conditioned examination framework and identify open research problems, including black box preservation, model version attestation, uncertainty quantification for surrogate based analysis, and chain of custody for mutable AI artifacts.

</details>


### [247] [A Challenge-Nonce Freshness Gap in Project Veraison's TPM Reference Schemes, Found by Appraising Application-Layer Action Evidence End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03534)
*Anton Sokolov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前将动作证据包（AEP）作为RATS架构下应用层证据的工作，首次将AEP做基端到端驱动通过符合规范的Project Veraison RATS验证器。作者发现并修复了一个安全漏洞：参考方案未强制挑战随机数新鲜度，导致重放引用仍被判定为肯定。修复后，同一合法引用在新会话中重放会从肯定翻转为禁忌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的AEP工作仅用最小化的验证器替身进行评价，未经过符合规范的端到端验证流程，其安全性和实用性缺乏充分验证。此外，AEP记录由受质疑的软件栈自身产生，签名日志只能证明哪个密钥写入记录，无法证明运行时完整性，需要硬件根信任的完整验证链。

Method: 在模拟软件TPM（swtpm）上生成EC P-256认证密钥，将AEP结果摘要测量到PCR中，生成真实引用并打包成Veraison的tpm-enacttrust格式，配置CReference Integrity Manifest（信任锚加黄金参考值），最终获得签名的EAT认证结果（EAR）。测试了合法证据、结果交换和单字节签名篡改三种场景。

Result: 良好证据产生肯定（affirming）结论；结果交换或单字节签名篡改产生禁忌（contraindicated）结论。发现参考方案未强制执行挑战随机数新鲜度，重放引用仍被判定为肯定。经过负责任披露后提出并在上游实现了一个两部分精确修复，验证修复后同一有效引用在重放至新会话时翻转为禁忌。

Conclusion: AEP + RATS的端到端管线在符合规范的Veraison验证器下验证有效，能够区分合法证据与被篡改/重放的证据。但基于软件TPM的模拟环境仅验证了逻辑正确性，不构成硬件安全保证；同时发现并修复了挑战随机数新鲜度缺失这一安全漏洞，为上游实现贡献了安全修复。

Abstract: When an automated agent (an AI agent, say) takes a consequential action, the record it leaves behind is produced by the very software stack whose integrity is in question. A signed log proves which key wrote the record, not what the runtime was. Prior work proposed treating that record (an Action Evidence Package, AEP: a signed append-only record of an action, its authorising principal, and its outcome) as application-layer Evidence under the IETF Remote ATtestation procedureS (RATS) architecture (RFC 9334), binding the outcome into a hardware-rooted TPM quote so that swapping it invalidates the quote. But that prior work appraised the result only against a minimal Verifier stand-in. This article closes that gap. We drive an AEP quote, produced on an emulated software TPM, end-to-end through a conformant Project Veraison RATS Verifier. We generate an EC P-256 attestation key, measure the AEP outcome digest into a PCR, pack a genuine quote into Veraison's tpm-enacttrust format, provision a Concise Reference Integrity Manifest (trust anchor plus golden reference value), and obtain a signed EAT Attestation Result (EAR). Good evidence yields affirming; an outcome-swap or a one-byte signature tamper yields contraindicated. Along the way we uncover, responsibly disclose, and fix a security-relevant finding: the reference scheme does not enforce challenge-nonce freshness, so a replayed quote still appraises as affirming. We give an exact, upstreamable two-part fix and validate it end-to-end. With the fix active, the same valid quote that is affirming in its own session flips to contraindicated when replayed to a fresh one. The pipeline is fully reproducible; the Attester remains an emulated swtpm, not a hardware guarantee.

</details>


### [248] [ReputationChain: Robust Trust Updating for Blockchain-Enabled Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03554)
*Adnan Iftekhar,Chengliang Zheng,Xiaohui Cui,Mir Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RC（Reputation and Collusion-resistant）参与者信任框架，将区块链作为证据与溯源层而非信任来源。通过有界证据转换、重复双边交互折扣、低对方多样性惩罚、身份置信度加权及基于验证交互量的中性先验衰减，有效抑制声誉通胀与合谋，降低身份分裂攻击影响，并改善稀疏历史诚实参与者的声誉公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链能保存供应链记录，但账本完整性本身无法证明参与者在未来风险敏感交易中是否值得信任。现有声誉系统主要关注产品证据、全局反馈聚合或评论真实性，却较少关注重复双边通胀、身份多重性（Sybil攻击）以及稀疏历史诚实参与者面临的非公平衰减三个核心问题。

Method: 将治理交互结果转化为有界证据；对同一对参与者的重复交互进行折扣；对低对方多样性施加惩罚；利用治理提供的身份置信度加权正面证据；声誉分数根据已验证交互量向中性先验衰减。身份、合约、结果及更新的溯源信息上链，非线性声誉计算在链下执行并在链上做可接受性检查。

Result: 在30次种子运行与匹配交互轨迹的受控模拟中，完整模型将平均合谋收益降至0.1443（对照：朴素均值证据0.3688，静态衰减0.3585）；单控制者操控十个身份时，声誉通胀比降至0.8723，而三个基线均高于1.08；在相同新参与者轨迹上，体积感知衰减将平均新参与者声誉从0.6626提升至0.7589，并将虚假低信任率从0.3633降至0.1683。配对分析证实跨运行改进显著。

Conclusion: 结果表明RC实现了对声誉失真的有界削减（而非攻击者检测）。在生产使用前仍需部署评估与运营数据校准。该工作的价值在于提供了一种区别于现有区块链声誉系统的信任框架设计思路，将链上溯源与链下计算相结合以抵御声誉操纵。

Abstract: Blockchain can preserve supply-chain records, but ledger integrity alone does not show whether a participant should be trusted in a future risk-sensitive transaction. Existing reputation systems mainly address product evidence, global feedback aggregation, or review authenticity, while giving less attention to repeated bilateral inflation, identity multiplicity, and unfair decay for honest participants with sparse histories. We present \RC, a participant trust framework that uses blockchain as an evidence and provenance layer rather than as the source of trust. Governed interaction outcomes are converted into bounded evidence. Repeated interactions between the same pair are discounted, low counterparty diversity is penalized, governance-supplied identity confidence weights positive evidence, and scores decay toward a neutral prior according to verified interaction volume. Identity, contract, outcome, and update provenance remain on chain, while nonlinear reputation computation is performed off chain and checked on chain for admissibility. In controlled simulations with 30 seeded runs and matched interaction traces, the full model reduces mean collusive gain to 0.1443, compared with 0.3688 for naive mean evidence and 0.3585 for static decay. With ten identities under one controller, the reputation inflation ratio falls to 0.8723, while three comparison baselines remain above 1.08. On identical newcomer traces, volume-aware decay increases mean newcomer reputation from 0.6626 to 0.7589 and reduces the false low-trust rate from 0.3633 to 0.1683. Paired analysis confirms these improvements across runs. The results support a bounded reduction in reputation distortion, not attacker detection. Deployment evaluation and calibration with operational data are still required before production use.

</details>


### [249] [CAN Disabler: Hardware-based Prevention method of Unauthorized Transmission in CAN and CAN-FD networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03567)
*Ryo Kurachi,T. David Pyun,Shinya Honda,Hiroaki Takada,Hiroshi Ueda,Satoshi Horihata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于改进CAN控制器的安全机制，通过阻止ECU以异常频率向CAN总线发送消息来阻断未经授权的CAN总线访问，并在CAN-FD总线上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来针对CAN总线的安全攻击事件频发，而当前车载控制网络中的ECU不具备任何安全功能。因此，车载控制网络迫切需要经济有效的安全防护措施。

Method: 对CAN控制器进行修改，使其能够阻止ECU以异常频率向CAN总线传输消息，从而阻断未经授权的CAN总线访问。该方法在CAN灵活数据速率（CAN-FD）总线上进行了实现与验证。

Result: 该方法在实际CAN-FD总线上成功验证了阻止异常频率消息传输的效果，证明了该基于控制器的禁用机制在CAN及CAN-FD网络中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于修改后CAN控制器的方法能够在不大幅增加成本的前提下，有效阻止ECU以异常频率向CAN总线发送消息，为车载控制网络提供了一种实用的安全防护手段。

Abstract: There have been a quite number of security attack cases against Controller Area Network (CAN) reported in recent years, but in the meantime no security function is included in the ECU in the current in-vehicle control network. Thus, in-vehicle control networks particularly require cost-effective security features. In this paper, therefore, we propose a method to block unauthorized CAN-bus access using a CAN controller that is modified to prevent the ECU from transmitting messages to the CAN-bus at abnormal frequency. Then, we have also demonstrated the effectiveness of the disabler on CAN with flexible data-rate (CAN-FD) buses.

</details>


### [250] [Secure Long-Range Autonomous Valet Parking: A Reservation Scheme With Three-Factor Authentication and Key Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03590)
*Di Wang,Yue Cao,Fei Yan,Yining Liu,Daxin Tian,Yuan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecLAVP是一个面向长距离自主代客泊车（LAVP）预约服务的可证明安全的三因子认证与密钥协商协议，结合密码、生物特征和智能卡，在乘客与自动驾驶车辆之间实现相互认证和会话密钥建立，以保障上下客过程中的身份合法性和通信安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAVP方案主要关注停车请求验证和泊车签到，未充分保护乘客上下车过程中的身份合法性和通信安全，存在安全漏洞。

Method: 提出SecLAVP协议，采用三因子认证（密码、生物特征、智能卡），借助上下客点（DP）辅助，使乘客与自动驾驶车辆（AV）完成相互认证并建立会话密钥；通过ROR模型进行形式化安全证明，使用AVISPA模拟进行安全性验证，并进行非正式安全分析与性能评估。

Result: 在ROR模型中证明了会话密钥安全性；AVISPA仿真表明能抵抗中间人攻击；非正式分析表明满足15项安全目标；性能评估表明在通信开销、计算开销和调度方面具有实际部署可行性。

Conclusion: SecLAVP为LAVP预约服务提供了一种安全可行的三因子认证方案，弥补了现有方案在上下客阶段身份合法性和通信安全方面的不足，具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Long-range autonomous valet parking (LAVP) is increasingly adopted to alleviate traffic congestion and parking difficulties. For large-scale parking demand, reservation can improve parking management. However, existing schemes mainly focus on parking request verification and parking check-in, and do not adequately protect identity legitimacy and communication security during passenger drop-off and pick-up. To address this problem, we propose SecLAVP, a provably secure three-factor authentication and key agreement protocol for LAVP reservation services. SecLAVP combines passwords, biometrics, and smart cards. With assistance from the drop-off/pick-up point (DP), the passenger and the autonomous vehicle (AV) achieve mutual authentication and establish a session key for secure communication. In the Real-Or-Random (ROR) model, we formally prove that SecLAVP provides session-key security. AVISPA simulations show that SecLAVP resists man-in-the-middle attacks, while informal analysis demonstrates that it satisfies 15 defined security goals. Finally, performance evaluation in terms of communication overhead, computational overhead, and scheduling shows that SecLAVP is feasible for practical deployment.

</details>


### [251] [DiagChain: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Evidence-Grounded Attack Chain Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03591)
*Xuyang Liu,Yibin Han,Zhenwei Zhang,Kai Chang,Zhiwei Xu,Tian Qiu,Weixian Deng,Jiabao Gao,Xiaolin Peng,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiagChain，一个用于证据驱动的攻击链重建任务的分阶段诊断基准，包含69个场景（MAIN-69）和证据中心RAG方法（ECRAG），通过5个互补指标对LLM代理进行系统性失败诊断。实验表明最强配置仅成功39.6%的参考步骤，且揭示了不同规模模型在不同阶段的不同瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理攻击链重建基准主要评估最终输出或总体准确率，无法提供中间推理阶段错误如何产生和传播的深入洞察，因此需要一种能够进行分阶段评估的诊断性基准。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) DiagChain基准框架，支持LLM代理在攻击链重建任务中的分阶段评估； (2) MAIN-69，包含69个覆盖多种操作系统、证据噪声水平和链长度的场景数据集；(3) ECRAG（Evidence-Centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation），将证据检索与重建链的演化结构化表示相结合；(4) 五个互补指标，用于评估重建过程的不同阶段并支持系统性失败诊断。

Result: 基于6种LLM的评估显示，最强配置在MAIN-69的849个参考步骤中仅成功39.6%。分析还发现，较小的模型在将检索到的证据纳入输出的基础任务上表现困难，而较大的模型能推进到后续步骤，但正确排序证据成为主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了超越端到端准确率的诊断性评估的重要性，并为改进证据驱动的网络安全代理提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer a promising approach to attack chain reconstruction by retrieving and interpreting heterogeneous telemetry to infer ordered attacker actions. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate final outputs or aggregate accuracy, providing limited insight into how errors arise and propagate across intermediate reasoning stages. We present DiagChain, a diagnostic benchmark for evidence-grounded attack chain reconstruction that enables stage-wise evaluation of LLM agents. DiagChain includes MAIN-69, a suite of 69 scenarios spanning multiple operating systems, evidence noise levels, and chain lengths. It further introduces Evidence-Centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (ECRAG), which couples evidence retrieval with an evolving structured representation of the reconstructed chain. Five complementary metrics are introduced to assess distinct stages of the reconstruction process and support systematic failure diagnosis. Based on evaluations using 6 LLMs, DiagChain reveals that even the strongest configuration succeeds on only 39.6% of the 849 reference steps in MAIN-69. Our analysis further shows that smaller models struggle with the more basic task of incorporating retrieved evidence into their outputs, whereas larger models can proceed to later steps, where correctly ordering that evidence becomes the main bottleneck. These results validate the importance of diagnostic evaluation beyond end-to-end accuracy and provide actionable insights for improving evidence-grounded cybersecurity agents.

</details>


### [252] [A Security-Oriented Lifecycle Model for Large Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03626)
*Eleftherios Batzolis,George Drosatos,Vassilis Katsouros,Konstantinos Rantos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向LLM系统的生命周期模型，围绕安全相关边界而非工作流优化来构建，包含32个阶段、4个核心管道层、12个LLMOps支柱和9个治理类别，并揭示当前监管框架存在结构性缺陷：治理证据集中于部署端，而最关键的安全决策发生在开发端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生命周期框架主要为运营效率设计，而非为安全分析设计；安全相关活动（如数据溯源验证、工件签名、代理权限控制、退役处理）往往被隐式对待或假设已得到适当关注。同时，治理框架按风险等级或管理流程组织需求，没有清晰关联到具体生命周期阶段。

Method: 提出一个支持安全分析的LLM生命周期模型，按安全相关边界而非工作流优化来划分阶段。模型包含4个核心管道层（数据、模型、分发、应用）共32个阶段，辅以12阶段的LLMOps支柱和9类别的治理支柱，并引入了13个新阶段以区分现有框架未明确的独立安全关注点。同时构建了融合NIST AI RMF、EU AI Act和ISO/IEC 42001的治理映射。

Result: 治理映射揭示了一个结构性属性：当前监管环境中的治理证据集中在部署面向阶段（系统对监管者可见之处），而最关键的决定——数据选择、对齐策略、能力边界——发生在开发面向阶段，恰好是监管可见性最低的地方。

Conclusion: 当前监管框架存在"部署端密集、开发端空白"的结构性失衡，需要在开发面向阶段加强治理可见性和要求，以覆盖安全风险影响最大的决策环节。

Abstract: Large language models are being integrated into critical infrastructure and enterprise workflows at unprecedented scale,yet the lifecycle frameworks governing their development and operations were designed for operational efficiency rather than security analysis. As a result, security-relevant activities such as data provenance verification, artifact signing, agentic permission control, and decommissioning are often left implicit or assumed to receive due care. Governance frameworks, in turn, organise requirements around risk levels or management processes without clearly linking them to the lifecycle stages where they apply. This paper addresses both deficiencies. We propose a lifecycle model for LLM systems that supports security analysis by structuring it around security-relevant boundaries rather than workflow optimisation. The model comprises 32 stages across four core pipeline layers (Data, Model, Distribution, Application), supported by a 12-stage LLMOps pillar and a 9-category governance pillar. Thirteen stages are introduced here as separate units because they expose distinct security concerns that existing frameworks do not clearly distinguish. A governance mapping synthesising the NIST AI RMF, the EU AI Act, and ISO/IEC 42001 reveals a structural property of the current regulatory landscape: governance evidence concentrates at deployment-facing stages, where systems are visible to regulators, while the most consequential decisions, data selection, alignment strategy, and capability boundaries, are made at development-facing stages, where regulatory visibility is lowest.

</details>


### [253] [Empirical Analysis of Evasion and Poisoning Against Malware Data Drift Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03642)
*Mingyue Yang,David Lie,Nicolas Papernot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对恶意软件分类器的逃避攻击和投毒攻击如何影响数据漂移检测器，并揭示了数据漂移检测器的独特特性使得针对恶意软件分类器的攻击在其上产生不同的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件演化导致的概念漂移给恶意软件分类带来挑战，机器学习驱动的数据漂移检测工具被开发用于缓解此问题。然而，现有研究未明确逃避攻击和投毒攻击如何同时作用于数据漂移检测器和恶意软件分类器组合。

Method: 系统性地研究逃避攻击与投毒攻击对数据漂移检测器及其搭配的恶意软件分类器的组合效果，演示数据漂移检测器的独特特性如何使针对恶意软件分类器的攻击在其上的行为产生差异。

Result: 证明数据漂移检测器的独特特性导致针对恶意软件分类器的攻击在数据漂移检测器上产生不同的攻击效果。

Conclusion: 需要针对数据漂移检测器与恶意软件分类器的组合重新审视攻击策略与防御机制。

Abstract: As concept drift due to malware evolution presents challenges for malware classification, machine learning-based data drift detection tools are developed to mitigate this problem. These data drift detector tools are designed for a different purpose and built with different techniques compared to malware classifiers. Although evasion and poisoning attacks against machine learning-based malware classifiers can cause misclassification of malware samples, it is not clear how these attacks work against data drift detectors and malware classifiers in combination. This work investigates the effect of evasion and poisoning attacks on the data drift detector along with the malware classifier. We demonstrate how unique characteristics of data drift detectors cause attacks against malware classifiers to work differently against them.

</details>


### [254] [When Agents Learn to Be You: Benchmarking Privacy Leakage, Impersonation Risk, and Defenses in Persona Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03700)
*Yongli Xiang,Zhifang Zhang,Bojun Yang,Ziming Hong,Lei Feng,Miao Xu,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AntiSkillBench，一个端到端基准，用于系统性评估角色技能（persona-skill）流水线中的隐私泄露与行为模仿风险。该基准包含7,500个由50个行为丰富的配置文件构建的角色对话轨迹、针对三种技能蒸馏策略的技能级隐私泄露和智能体级属性披露/行为模仿评估套件，以及四种在线/事后防御配置的评估。实验表明风险在多个前沿智能体和蒸馏协议中持续存在，且现有防御效果有限且依赖蒸馏策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色技能将个人交互历史蒸馏为可移植、可执行的工件供下游智能体使用，虽然实现了灵活的个性化，但这一过程将碎片化的个人信号集中化、通过复用放大其影响，且挑战了为单个记录或基于检索的记忆设计的现有防御机制。目前缺乏系统研究角色技能流水线安全性的基准。

Method: 构建AntiSkillBench基准，包含：(i) 由50个行为丰富配置文件构建的7,500个角色对话轨迹数据集；(ii) 一个评估套件，在三种技能蒸馏策略下测量技能级隐私泄露、智能体级属性披露和行为模仿；(iii) 一个覆盖在线和事后干预的四种防御配置的防御评估，包括主动风险抑制和被动来源保护。

Result: 在三个前沿智能体上的实验显示，角色技能风险在所有智能体骨干和蒸馏协议中持续存在，风险从显式属性扩展到沟通风格和人格特质。现有防御表现出有限且依赖蒸馏策略的有效性，无法跨风险和蒸馏策略泛化。

Conclusion: AntiSkillBench是开发隐私保护和真实性感知的角色技能的一个具有挑战性的基准，揭示了现有防御的不足，并强调了角色技能流水线中安全问题的普遍性与复杂性。

Abstract: Persona skills distill personal interaction histories into portable and executable artifacts for downstream agents. While enabling flexible personalization, this process concentrates fragmented personal signals, amplifies their impact through reuse, and challenges defenses designed for individual records or retrieval-based memory. To systematically investigate the safety of the persona-skill pipeline, we introduce AntiSkillBench, an end-to-end benchmark for evaluating risks and defenses across the persona-skill pipeline. It comprises: (i) a dataset of 7,500 persona-grounded dialogue traces, constructed from 50 behaviorally rich profiles spanning diverse task scenarios; (ii) an evaluation suite that measures skill-level privacy leakage and agent-level attribute disclosure and behavioral impersonation across three skill-distillation strategies; and (iii) a defense evaluation covering four configurations across online and post-hoc interventions, including active risk suppression and passive provenance protection. Experiments across three frontier agents show that persona-skill risks persist across agent backbones and distillation protocols, extending from explicit attributes to communication styles and personality traits. Existing defenses exhibit limited and distillation-dependent effectiveness, failing to generalize across risk and distillation strategies. These results highlight AntiSkillBench as a challenging benchmark for developing privacy-preserving and authenticity-aware persona skills.

</details>


### [255] [Dependency Triad: A Metric to Quantify the Dependencies Between Attributes for Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03737)
*Sandaru Jayawardana,Sennur Ulukus,Ming Ding,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Dependency Triad"(DT)的新型度量指标，用于估计多维数据收集场景下由属性间相关性引发的隐私泄露(CPL)。DT用三个参数摘要配对依赖信息，实现常数时间的CPL保守估计，并能显式建模先验知识的不确定性，对高基数属性和稀疏分布具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多维数据收集场景中，属性间的依赖关系可能无意中泄露关联信息并增加隐私风险。然而现有的CPL分析方法要么需要精确的先验知识，要么在大规模属性数和高峰属性的场景下面临可扩展性挑战，限制了其在实际数据中的适用性。

Method: 提出DT(Dependency Triad)度量：用三个参数概括与CPL相关的配对依赖信息，提供常数时间的配对CPL保守估计；通过参数显式建模先验分布知识的不确定性，保证泄露估计的鲁棒性；以配对形式作为构建模块，用于评估多维场景中的总体泄露。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，DT在不同依赖机制和先验不确定性条件下均能一致地估计CPL。

Conclusion: DT作为一种新度量，解决了现有CPL分析在可扩展性和鲁棒性上的不足，能在多维数据收集场景中提供高效、可靠的隐私泄露估计，兼顾隐私分析与隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: Collecting multidimensional user data is essential for extracting rich insights across various applications. Local Differential Privacy (LDP) has emerged as a de facto standard for mitigating privacy risks in such scenarios. A key challenge in privacy-preserving multidimensional data collection lies in inter-attribute dependencies, as they can inadvertently reveal correlated information and increase privacy vulnerabilities. Therefore, accurately measuring correlation-induced privacy leakage (CPL) is essential for privacy analysis and privacy-utility trade-off. However, existing CPL analysis solutions either require accurate prior knowledge or face scalability challenges for large numbers of attributes and high-cardinality attributes. These limit their practical applicability in real data. To address this research gap, we propose a novel metric, ``Dependency Triad'' (DT), which summarizes the pairwise dependency information relevant to CPL using three parameters and yields a \emph{constant-time} conservative estimator of pairwise CPL. DT explicitly models uncertainty in prior distributional knowledge through its parameters, delivering robust leakage estimates. Moreover, its robustness to sparse distributions makes it particularly suitable for high-cardinality attributes, while the pairwise formulation serves as a tractable building block for assessing total leakage in multidimensional settings. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that DT consistently estimates CPL across diverse dependency regimes and prior uncertainties.

</details>


### [256] [Delay Attacks on the German Smart Metering Infrastructure: A Security Analysis of CLS Channel Timing Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03751)
*Fabio Stoll,Benjamin Pottkamp,Heiko Lorenz,Shalaka Kale,Jessica Rövekamp,Joachim Gerlach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了德国智能计量基础设施中可控本地系统（CLS）通道控制信号遭受延迟攻击的可行性，结合理论分析与实验验证。结果表明，具备足够上下文知识的WAN路径攻击者可实现延迟攻击，理论上限约为48小时；若同时对数十万个CLS设备发起攻击，可能导致显著的频率偏差并引发甩负荷。文章还提出了缓解策略，包括SMGW配置限制、实现级修改和协议扩展（如TLS 1.3扩展）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国智能计量基础设施（SMI）通过可控本地系统（CLS）通道传输电网控制信号，该通道的安全性直接关系到电力系统的稳定运行。作者希望评估延迟攻击（delay attacks）在该通道上的可行性，以及多通道同时被攻击时对电网的潜在影响，从而为安全加固提供依据。

Method: 结合理论分析与实验验证：在符合Common Criteria Protection Profile（针对SMGW）的威胁模型下，分析WAN中on-path攻击者实施延迟攻击的理论上限（约48小时）；同时将单一CLS攻击扩展到数十万台CLS设备，评估对电网频率偏差和甩负荷的影响；并针对不同层级（SMGW配置、实现层、协议层）提出差异化缓解方案。

Result: 1) WAN中的on-path攻击者在具备足够上下文知识时，可以可行地执行延迟攻击，部分部署协议配置的理论上限约为48小时；2) 从单一CLS扩展到数十万个CLS，攻击者可引发显著的频率偏差并可能导致甩负荷；3) 攻击扩展需要针对每个目标实现和配置的上下文知识，其可复用性不确定，但随标准化推进可能更易获得；4) FNN Steuerbox和CLS.EEDI应用中的时间限制传输是实现特定的，可由制造商解决；TLS 1.3中的应用数据时间限制需要协议级扩展，所提出的TL扩展可在保持向后兼容的同时提供可持续缓解。

Conclusion: 德国SMI的CLS通道对延迟攻击存在现实且可被利用的漏洞，尤其在WAN中的on-path攻击者具备足够上下文知识时。通过组合攻击大量CLS通道可对电网稳定性造成显著威胁。缓解措施需分层实施：制造商解决实现特定问题，协议层面通过TLS扩展实现可持续防护。SMI之外的其他通信通道（如专有RTU）不在本文范围内，但可能存在类似或更严重的漏洞。

Abstract: This work analyzes the feasibility of delay attacks on control signals transmitted via the Controllable Local System (CLS) channel of the German Smart Metering Infrastructure (SMI). It combines theoretical analysis with experimental validation under a threat model aligned to the Common Criteria Protection Profile for the Smart Meter Gateway (SMGW) and assess the potential impact on the power grid if the identified attack vector is exploited across multiple CLS channels simultaneously. We also outline mitigation strategies, including SMGW configuration restrictions, implementation-level changes, and protocol extensions.
  Our results show that an on-path attacker in the Wide Area Network (WAN) with sufficient contextual knowledge can feasibly execute delay attacks, with a theoretical upper bound of roughly 48 hours for some deployed protocol configurations. Projecting from a single CLS to several hundred thousand CLS devices indicates such an adversary could cause a significant frequency deviation potentially resulting in load shedding. Scaling the attack requires contextual knowledge for each targeted implementation and configuration; whether this knowledge can be broadly reused across CLS channels is uncertain but may become easier to obtain as standardization progresses.
  Time restricted transmissions in the FNN Steuerbox and in applications using CLS.EEDI are implementation-specific and can therefore be addressed by manufacturers. By contrast, ensuring application-data time limitations in TLS~1.3 requires protocol-level extensions. The TLS extensions proposed here offer a sustainable mitigation while preserving backward compatibility.
  Other communication channels outside the SMI (for example, proprietary remote terminal units used to control a CLS) are outside this work's scope and may exhibit similar or worse vulnerabilities.

</details>
